Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [60] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出C4框架，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的跨概念创造力理解能力，构建了C4-Eval评测集，实验显示最强闭源模型准确率仅约50%，开源模型更低，揭示了当前MLLMs在解码跨概念创造性编码含义方面存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估MLLMs的创造性能力在设计、沟通、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但因缺乏明确目标和奖励信号而难以量化；跨概念理解是感知创造力的核心认知能力，使感知者能从非显而易见但有意义的概念关系中恢复意图含义。

Method: 提出基于认知的C4评估框架，将项目构建操作化为跨概念编码，模型推理操作化为跨概念解码。编码组件在人工标注并经第三方审核的跨概念网络中，将目标槽映射为沿桥接路径的可视化替代概念，支持批量生成、显式结构、按桥接数和深度索引难度、提供精确答案。构建C4-Eval评测集，包含184个合成项和37个人工创建的跨概念成语图形，人工构建并审核跨概念关系、桥接路径和推理过程。每个项实例化为五种任务设置，产生884个主要答案恢复案例。评测十个MLLMs。

Result: 最强闭源模型主要准确率达50.7%和48.0%，开源模型显著更低。候选约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥接提示和解释请求仅带来适度增益。结果暴露当前MLLMs在通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码含义方面存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: C4框架为评估MLLMs的跨概念创造力理解提供了可扩展、结构化且可控难度的基准，揭示了现有模型在创造性解码上的根本局限，为未来改进指明方向。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [61] [DocMemo: Dynamic Evidence Discovery via Probabilistic Memory-Guided Retrieval for Multi-Modal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07067)
*Hanshu Yao,Janfeng Zhong,Niu Lian,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocMemo提出了一个基于记忆引导的长文档推理框架，通过三级检索状态（文档模式记忆、页面信念记忆、问题情景记忆）实现动态证据探索，结合贝叶斯页面信念更新与Thompson采样、空间邻近传播、结构感知自适应粒度证据访问，在三个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档理解系统受限于静态检索和脆弱的跨轮记忆：单轮方法预先固定top-k页面集合，难以从早期检索错误恢复；迭代方法虽允许多轮证据获取，但未研究跨轮状态传播机制，难以追踪页面相关性的动态变化。

Method: 提出DocMemo框架，维护三级检索状态：文档模式记忆捕获结构先验，页面信念记忆估计动态相关性，问题情景记忆记录查询特定推理轨迹。推理过程中通过贝叶斯页面信念更新与Thompson采样、空间邻近传播、结构感知自适应粒度证据访问持续优化跨轮页面选择，并辅以细粒度视觉区域补充页面级证据。

Result: 在3个基准实验中，DocMemo达到最先进性能，验证了结构化记忆和动态页面信念更新的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: DocMemo通过三级记忆架构和动态信念更新机制，有效解决了长文档推理中的静态检索与跨轮记忆脆弱问题，实现了动态证据探索，显著提升了长文档理解性能。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires locating sparse and heterogeneous evidence across hundreds of pages, yet existing systems remain limited by static retrieval and fragile cross-round memory. Mainstream single-round methods commit to a fixed top-$k$ page set at the outset and struggle to recover from early retrieval errors; recent iterative approaches allow multi-round evidence acquisition, but they do not investigate the propagation mechanism of cross-round states, making it difficult to track the dynamic changes in page relevance. To address these limitations, we propose DocMemo, a memory-guided framework that formulates long-document reasoning as dynamic evidence exploration. DocMemo maintains a tri-level retrieval state consisting of Document Schema Memory, Page Belief Memory, and Question Episodic Memory, which respectively capture structural priors, dynamic relevance estimation, and query-specific reasoning trajectories. During reasoning, DocMemo continuously refines cross-round page selection through Bayesian page belief updating with Thompson sampling, spatial proximity propagation, and structure-aware adaptive-granularity evidence access, while supplementing page-level evidence with fine-grained visual regions. Experiments on 3 benchmarks show that DocMemo achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the efficacy of structured memory and dynamic page belief updating. Code is available at https://github.com/Harrygof/DocMemo.

</details>


### [62] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 KnowPlan 系统，将学位规划分为两个阶段：CatalogBrowse 从异构官方源中提取课程目录，DegreeMap 基于提取结果优化个性化学习路径。CatalogBrowse 通过基于边际增益的探索、确定性解析和闭包证书终止，以 47% 更少的源访问实现 96.2% 目录召回率；DegreeMap 利用 CP-SAT 在硬约束、学期跨度、负载风险、个性化效用和选项价值上字典序优化，保持 100% 硬可行性并提升效用 0.066。完整流水线对 99.5% 请求可认证，与特权金标准图的效用差距仅 0.015。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学位规划系统将课程目录抽取与个性化路径优化耦合，导致抽取器仅学习当前计划所需的知识，无法发现完整的课程需求；且异构源（目录、网页、JSON、PDF）缺乏统一模式，使得抽取与优化的接口模糊，难以保证正确性和可认证性。

Method: KnowPlan 强制抽取优先边界：1) CatalogBrowse 无用户画像探索，按原子目录义务的低置信度期望边际增益/源访问成本打分动作，通过平台适配器确定性解析并回退到跨度约束的子句到 AST 模型，以索引、模式、溯源、引用完整性的闭包证书终止，输出三个带溯源的 JSON 文档。2) DegreeMap 仅消费这些文档，编译为类型化需求超图，用 CP-SAT 按硬可行性、完成期限、负载风险、个性化效用、选项价值字典序优化，每阶段在前一阶段已证最优内优化并保持在求解器预算内可认证。

Result: 在 100 校广度轨道和 6 校深度轨道上：CatalogBrowse 达到 96.2% 目录召回率和 88.7% 掩码源恢复率，源访问量比全量爬虫降低 47%；DegreeMap 保持 100% 硬可行性，个性化效用比最强基线提升 0.066；完整流水线 99.5% 请求可认证，与特权金标准图的效用差距为 0.015。

Conclusion: KnowPlan 通过解耦抽取与优化、引入可验证的接口契约和字典序 CP-SAT 优化，实现了高召回、低访问成本的课程目录重建，以及硬约束下可认证的个性化学位规划，显著优于现有耦合方法。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [63] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskSense提出了一种以任务为中心的世界模型框架，通过可微分随机空间注意力机制和逆动力学辅助目标，使模型关注控制相关区域，忽略视觉干扰，在DeepMind Control Suite保持竞争力，在Distracting Control Suite显著优于DreamerV3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通过重建观测学习潜在状态，隐式保留整个视觉输入信息，但任务相关内容仅占小部分，背景干扰消耗表示容量，导致潜在表示偏向任务无关内容，降低下游控制性能。

Method: TaskSense引入基于前一潜在状态条件的可微分随机空间注意力机制，在编码前强制任务相关性；辅以逆动力学目标引导注意力关注控制相关区域；世界模型仅重建被关注区域而非全观测，解码器再条件化于采样的注意力图以保证一致性重建。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上与DreamerV3性能持平，在Distracting Control Suite上持续超越DreamerV3，展示对视觉干扰的鲁棒性显著提升；定性分析确认学习到的注意力在逆动力学监督下稳定定位控制相关区域并抑制无关内容。

Conclusion: TaskSense通过任务中心的注意力机制和逆动力学引导，有效解决了世界模型中视觉重建与控制目标的错位，提升了视觉干扰下的控制鲁棒性，为视觉控制中的表示学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content. The decoder is further conditioned on the sampled attention map, enabling consistent reconstruction despite stochastic attention. Compared with the DreamerV3 baseline, TaskSense maintains competitive performance on the DeepMind Control Suite while consistently outperforming DreamerV3 on the Distracting Control Suite, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to visual distractions. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the learned attention, guided by inverse-dynamics supervision, consistently localizes control-relevant regions while suppressing irrelevant visual content.

</details>


### [64] [GeoBenchLLM: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on Geo-Related Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07411)
*Rodrigo Ferreira Rodrigues,Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoBenchLLM，一个用于评估大语言模型在geo相关任务上表现的综合基准，包含12个多样化的公开数据集，实验表明推理能力和模型规模对整体性能有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在地理数据领域常在同质化设置下被研究，这极大地限制了对其泛化能力的洞察。

Method: 精心选择了12个来自不同geo相关任务和领域的公开数据集，构建综合基准GeoBenchLLM，并在此基准上评估一组LLMs的地理空间和时间理解能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，推理能力和模型规模对整体性能有强烈影响。

Conclusion: GeoBenchLLM已公开发布（https://github.com/Rfr2003/GeoBenchLLM），为探测LLMs在geo相关任务上的能力提供了综合基准。

Abstract: In the context of geodata, existing Large Language Models have often been studied in a homogeneous setting, which has considerably limited insights into their generalization capabilities. In this paper, we present \benchName, a comprehensive benchmark for probing LLMs on geo-related tasks. We leverage a careful selection of twelve publicly available datasets from diverse geo-related tasks and domains, and evaluate a set of LLMs on geo-spatial and temporal understanding using our benchmark. Our results show that reasoning and size have a strong impact on overall performance. GeoBenchLLM is publicly available at https://github.com/Rfr2003/GeoBenchLLM.

</details>


### [65] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估六个前沿模型在显式引导下的行为可控性，发现模型不仅在引导幅度上不同，还在响应模式上存在差异，部分模式仅出现在特定模型；通过线性探针和激活干预在Llama中定位了行为分裂的内部机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同训练数据、目标和安全管线导致的前沿模型在显式引导压力下的行为差异，填补此前研究不足。

Method: 使用六个开发者的六个前沿模型，构建300对基础与引导条目（三类：价值观冲突、推理引出、推理抑制）加40验证条目；模型作为盲评裁判基于固定行为量表分类响应，共24,480次判断，采用留一法共识评分；在Llama上进行线性探针解码残留流，并注入方向进行生成干预。

Result: 模型在引导下的响应模式存在显著差异：GPT-5以99%比例拒绝透露推理但保留答案（其他模型0%）；Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令；Llama内部最大行为分裂可被线性探针以0.87保留准确率解码，注入该方向可将行为从0%驱动至86%；所有发现在token预算补救和盲假设判断提示控制实验下均成立。

Conclusion: 前沿模型在显式引导下表现出独特的行为模式差异，这些差异可追溯到模型内部表示；开放权重模型允许通过激活工程定位和调控行为，为模型可控性研究提供新见解。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


### [66] [Automated item evaluation: Predicting item acceptance and rejection using LLM-generated critiques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06609)
*Hotaka Maeda,Yikai Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于文本的自动化题目评估(AIE)模型，利用DeBERTaV3-large和融合模型预测题目接受/拒绝，数学题目预测效果优于英语语言艺术(ELA)，融合模型整体表现最佳(AUC=0.80)，但在识别偏见、敏感性、公平性和可访问性问题上表现不足，尤其在ELA领域。降低决策阈值可提高灵敏度但降低特异性，适用于自动生成题目场景。研究表明基于文本的AIE在某些领域可行，可减轻人工审核负担，但涉及公平性问题仍需人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个近乎全面的自动化题目评估(AIE)模型，利用大规模标准化考试项目的历史拒绝数据，仅通过题目文本预测题目接受或拒绝，以减少人工专家审核和现场测试的负担。

Method: 使用包含52,759道英语语言艺术(ELA)和数学题目的数据集，其中34%被永久拒绝。拒绝原因包括心理测量属性差、内容问题、偏见和敏感性顾虑及非内容问题。微调DeBERTaV3-large分类器处理原始题目文本，第二个DeBERTa分类器处理Qwen3生成的题目评析，并构建融合模型结合两者表征。

Result: 融合模型整体性能最佳(Accuracy=0.75, F1=0.64, AUC=0.80, Sensitivity=0.64, Specificity=0.81)。数学题目预测(F1=0.73, AUC=0.86)显著优于ELA(F1=0.51, AUC=0.72)。将决策阈值从0.5降至0.25，使ELA和数学的平均灵敏度分别提升至0.88和0.91，但特异性分别降至0.31和0.56。引入题目评析改善了大多数拒绝原因的预测。模型给难度更高的题目分配了更高拒绝概率。但融合模型难以识别因偏见、敏感性、公平性或可访问性被标记的题目，尤其在ELA中。

Conclusion: 基于文本的AIE在某些领域可行，可作为减轻人工审核和现场测试负担的实用工具，但也凸显了对于涉及公平性顾虑的题目，人工审查仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Automated item evaluation (AIE) refers to the use of computational methods to assess item quality without requiring manual expert review or field testing of the items under evaluation. We aimed to build a near-comprehensive AIE model by predicting item acceptance and rejection from item text using historical rejection data from a large-scale standardized testing program. The dataset contained 52,759 English language arts (ELA) and mathematics items with 34% permanently rejected from future operational use. Rejection reasons included poor psychometric properties, content issues, bias and sensitivity concerns, and non-content issues. We fine-tuned a DeBERTaV3-large classifier on raw item text, a second DeBERTa classifier on Qwen3-generated item critiques, and a fusion model combining representations from both. The fusion model achieved the strongest overall performance (Accuracy = .75, F1 = .64, AUC = .80, Sensitivity = .64, Specificity = .81). Prediction for math (F1 = .73, AUC = .86) was considerably more accurate than ELA (F1 = .51, AUC = .72). Lowering the decision threshold from .5 to .25 raised average sensitivity for ELA and math to .88 and .91, while reducing specificity to .31 and .56, respectively, which may be preferable in automated item generation contexts where generating items is cheaper than evaluating them. Incorporating item critiques alongside raw item text improved performance across most rejection reasons. The model assigned higher rejection probabilities to more difficult items. However, the fusion model struggled to identify items flagged for bias, sensitivity, fairness, or accessibility, especially for ELA. These findings suggest that text-based AIE is feasible in some areas and may offer a practical tool for reducing the burden of manual review and field testing, while also underscoring the importance of human review for items with fairness concerns.

</details>


### [67] [NxN E-valuation: Hypothesis Certification via a Conformal CRT Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06621)
*Bin Wang,Yan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NxN E-valuation，一种基于e-value的假设认证算法，利用大数据集中不同样本互为零假设，实现条件随机化测试（CRT），无需针对性构建零假设，适用于LLM探索系统，解决幻觉问题，优于循环验证和留出测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在探索系统中擅长提出假设但存在严重幻觉，现有补救措施（如循环验证、留出测试）各有不足，无法可靠认证假设。

Method: NxN E-valuation利用天然存在的大规模训练集，让不同样本相互作为零假设，直接实现条件随机化测试（CRT），基于e-value进行假设认证，无需构建专用零假设。

Result: 该方法可作为LLM循环验证和留出数据测试的通用更优替代，前提是LLM生成的假设适用于每个单独样本。

Conclusion: NxN E-valuation提供了一种通用、无需针对性设计的假设认证框架，有效解决LLM探索中的幻觉问题，具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose NxN E-valuation, a handy, e-value-based hypothesis-certification algorithm that lets a hypothesis be verified without building any case-specific certification procedure---such as constructing a dedicated null hypothesis---as long as a large enough dataset is available. The method is especially suited to LLM-based exploration systems, where LLMs are remarkably good at proposing hypotheses but suffer badly from hallucination; this hallucination prevents us from harvesting LLM outputs directly, and existing remedies each fall short. The most common solutions include letting the LLM verify or correct itself circular verification and held-out testing (where false hypotheses can still pass via spurious correlations), among other remedies detailed in the introduction. To resolve this, NxN E-valuation exploits the naturally existing large training set and lets different samples serve as null hypotheses for one another. This design directly realizes a conditional randomization test (CRT) that certifies each hypothesis. The approach can be a universally better replacement for at least LLM circular verification and held-out-data testing, provided the LLM's generations are hypotheses that apply to each individual sample.

</details>


### [68] [Shape Your Feed: An LLM-based Agentic System for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06632)
*Ziyun Xu,Bosen Ding,Yue Zhang,Ji Qi,Qingyuan Song,Jizhou Huang,Liwei Wang,Jefferey Santelli,Yue Weng,Qichao Que,Zhenheng Yang,Junfeng Pan,Linhong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Shape Your Feed (SYF)，一种基于 LLM 的智能体推荐框架，通过三层架构（感知流、服务流、自演化流）实现实时、多模态的用户协同策展，解决被动推荐与用户显式意图的差距。离线实验对齐评分准确率达 98.85%，在线 A/B 实验显著提升 Feed 相关性与用户情绪，验证了工业级可落地的交互式可控推荐路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业推荐系统主要采用被动排序范式，依赖隐式行为信号（点击、停留时长）推断偏好，导致用户显式兴趣与算法分发内容存在持续偏差，用户难以表达细微偏好或实时干预 Feed。

Method: SYF 采用三层架构：(i) 感知流：从文本提示、语音指令、UI 交互捕捉细粒度用户意图；(ii) 服务流：基于持久化语义画像进行实时智能体重排与剪枝；(iii) 自演化流：通过直接偏好优化 (DPO) 与 LLM-as-a-Judge 集成，将系统行为与人类判断对齐。

Result: 离线评估中对齐评分模块准确率达 98.85%，大幅超越强 few-shot 基线；大规模在线 A/B 实验表明 SYF 显著提升 Feed 相关性与用户情绪指标。

Conclusion: SYF 证明了在工业环境中构建可交互、用户可控的推荐系统具有实用性与可扩展性，为从被动推荐向主动协同策展转型提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems predominantly adopt a passive ranking paradigm that infers user preferences from implicit behavioral signals (e.g., clicks, dwell time) rather than explicit, natural language inputs. As a result, users experience a persistent discrepancy between their explicit interests and what passive behavioral algorithms deliver, limiting their ability to express nuanced preferences or steer their feed in real time. To address this growing gap between how recommendations are optimized and how users wish to articulate their interests, we present Shape Your Feed (SYF), an LLM-based agentic recommendation framework that enables real-time, multimodal co-curation of content. SYF employs a three-tier architecture: (i) a Perception Flow that captures fine-grained user intent from text prompts, voice commands, and UI interactions; (ii) a Serving Flow that performs real-time agentic re-ranking and pruning of candidate items, grounded in a persistent Semantic Profile encoding evolving user preferences; and (iii) a Self-Evolution Flow that aligns system behavior with human judgments via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and an LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble. Offline evaluations show that SYF's alignment scoring module achieves 98.85% accuracy, substantially improving over strong few-shot baselines. Large-scale online A/B experiments on production traffic further demonstrate that SYF improves feed relevance and user sentiment, indicating a practical and scalable path toward interactive, user-steerable recommendation in industrial settings.

</details>


### [69] [TRACE: A Multi-Layer Benchmark for Human AI Controller Coordination Under Drift and Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06657)
*Joshua Zuniga,Srinivasan Subramanian,Ramya Madhuri Narapureddy,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于ALFRED的多层执行轨迹基准，注入受控漂移，提供1918条带标注的漂移轨迹，并提出无泄露评估协议，基线实验显示漂移可被识别和归因，注意力模型无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络物理和AI辅助系统将人类操作员、AI决策模块和自动控制器耦合在单一控制回路中，可信度取决于整个回路而非单一模型。缺乏标准基准捕捉时间对齐的多层轨迹，无法诊断协调故障的位置、原因及恢复方法。本文聚焦漂移问题，漂移可源自任何栈层，常规单模态监测无法定位层或确定起始时间。

Method: 基于ALFRED基准注入受控漂移，生成1918条漂移轨迹。每条轨迹为五个执行层（状态、观测、决策、规则、控制）的时间对齐逐步记录序列，标注漂移类型、影响层、起始时间、责任主体、因果机制，经独立标注员验证并报告一致性。配合无泄露评估协议消除起始时间泄露，跨经典、循环、注意力模型家族进行基线研究。

Result: 在诚实协议下，漂移在所有模型家族中均显著优于随机和多数类基线：影响层宏观F1约0.70，责任主体约0.85，因果机制约0.49。重注意力模型在此符号基准上不比简单模型有优势。

Conclusion: 提出的多层漂移基准和无泄露协议使漂移识别和归因成为可能，揭示了简单模型在符号任务中可与复杂注意力模型媲美，为多层系统可信性诊断提供了评估基础。

Abstract: Modern cyber-physical and AI-assisted systems couple human operators, AI decision modules, and automated controllers in a single control loop, so trustworthiness depends on the whole loop, not any one model. Yet no standard benchmark captures time-aligned, multi-layer traces of how drift and failures propagate across these layers, so we cannot diagnose where coordination breaks down, why, or how to recover. This paper targets one facet of that gap: drift, a deviation that can originate in any stack layer and that conventional single-modality monitoring cannot localize to a layer or pin to an onset time. We construct a benchmark by injecting controlled drift into traces derived from ALFRED, a grounded-instruction benchmark for everyday household tasks, yielding 1,918 drifted traces. Each trace is a time-aligned sequence of per-step records across five execution layers (state, observation, decision, rules, control), labeled with the drift type, affected layer, onset time, responsible actor, and causal mechanism, and validated by independent raters with inter-annotator agreement reported. We pair the dataset with a leak-aware protocol that removes a near-perfect onset leak, and a baseline study across classical, recurrent, and attention-based model families. Under this honest protocol, drift is identifiable and attributable well above random and majority baselines across every family (affected layer macro-F1 near 0.70, responsible actor near 0.85, causal mechanism near 0.49), and heavy attention offers no advantage over simpler models on this symbolic benchmark.

</details>


### [70] [CellWorld: From Gene-Level Reconstruction to Latent Cell Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06659)
*Haiping Liu,Qian Zhao,Lijing Lin,Jingyuan Sun,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CellWorld通过潜在空间预测式预训练构建空间转录组学基础模型，避免重构测序技术噪声，在仅5.74M参数下即超越所有基线，且冻结的大模型在仅用5%数据预训练时仍能击败全微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组学基础模型主要重构掩码基因身份或表达值，可能导致模型学习到检测特有的技术变异，限制了表示的可迁移性。

Method: 提出CellWorld，将预测目标从观测基因测量转移到潜在细胞表示，利用可见空间上下文和部分表达提示预测掩码细胞的潜在表示。在4600万人类细胞语料上预训练四个变体（5.74M-94.56M参数），并进行受控扩展实验。

Result: 性能随模型容量提升，空间任务受益最大；空间迁移更依赖充分优化和广泛生物学来源多样性而非单纯细胞数量。CellWorld-Small在所有11个线性探针和7个微调空间基准上均超越基线；冻结的CellWorld-Large仅用5%语料且覆盖广泛生物来源预训练，即在所有7个空间基准上击败全微调基线。

Conclusion: 潜在空间预测式预训练为空间转录组学提供了可扩展的基础模型路径，通过避免直接重构技术变异并利用空间上下文，实现了参数高效、数据高效且泛化能力强的表示学习。

Abstract: This paper shows that latent-space predictive pretraining can provide a scalable route to foundation models for spatial transcriptomics. Existing spatial transcriptomics foundation models primarily reconstruct masked gene identities or expression values, potentially encouraging the reproduction of assay-specific technical variation and limiting representation transferability. To avoid directly reconstructing such variation, we shift the prediction target from observed gene measurements to latent cell representations and introduce CellWorld, which predicts the latent representations of masked cells from visible spatial context and a limited partial-expression hint. We pretrain four CellWorld variants, spanning 5.74M to 94.56M trainable parameters, on a corpus of 46 million human cells. Our controlled scaling experiments show that performance improves with model capacity, particularly on spatial tasks, while spatial transfer depends more on sufficient optimization and broad biological source diversity than on cell count alone. Across four held-out datasets, even CellWorld-Small, with 5.74M trainable parameters, outperforms every baseline on all 11 linear-probe benchmarks and all seven fine-tuned spatial benchmarks. Most notably, a frozen CellWorld-Large pretrained on only 5\% of the corpus with broad biological source coverage outperforms every fully fine-tuned baseline across all seven spatial benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/UoM-HealthAI/CellWorld.

</details>


### [71] [Vehicle routing problem using deep reinforcement learning - A case study about truck planning in the industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06668)
*Siliang Lu,Dan Hu,Lili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三个实际物流案例研究，展示了基于深度强化学习(DRL)的车辆路径优化在外部卡车网络设计中的应用，实现了比基线方案降低10%以上的总成本，并提出未来可将DRL算法推广到更多VRP变体中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的车辆路径问题(VRP)面临复杂约束、信息不透明、不确定性和人类非理性行为等挑战，传统数学模型在实际部署中难以保持最优结果，因此需要探索基于深度强化学习的解决方案。

Method: 采用深度强化学习(DRL)算法进行车辆路径优化，通过三个不同的物流用例研究(涉及外部卡车网络设计)在工业场景中实施和验证该方法。

Result: 强化学习智能体优化的路线相比基线结果实现了超过10%的总成本降低，验证了DRL在实际工业场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文证明了基于DRL的车辆路径优化可成功应用于实际物流场景，并建议未来研究将DRL算法推广到更多VRP变体问题中，以解决更广泛的路径优化挑战。

Abstract: As an important component of the supply chain industry, transportation has experienced rapid development in the past decade with the assistance of digital platforms and intelligent algorithms. Within the field of transportation research, Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP) has remained a persistent and enduring challenge. In the realm of management science, experts, and scholars from both the industrial and academic sectors have continuously explored optimization models and algorithms to effectively address routing problems, from the classical Traveling Salesman Problem to the more general Vehicle Routing Problem. These models and algorithms are applied in real-world industrial scenarios to achieve cost optimization and reduce carbon footprints. However, due to the complexity of real-world problems, numerous specific constraints are often added, and challenges such as information opacity, uncertainty, and irrational human behavior may arise. Therefore, deploying and optimizing mathematical models for VRP in practical scenarios while maintaining optimal results poses numerous challenges. This paper discusses and provides solutions for three different logistic use cases involving external truck network design. Through these industrial case study, the paper introduces how deep reinforcement learning-based vehicle routing optimization has been implemented. As a result, it can be observed that the routes optimized by reinforcement learning agent have over 10% total cost compared to baseline results. Furthermore, the paper proposes that in future research, DRL algorithms for vehicle routing problems could be generalized into more variations of VRP.

</details>


### [72] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics of Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06694)
*Joohee Choi,Junhyeong Lee,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CGMas 是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，能够从自然语言描述自动完成聚合物粗粒化建模的全流程，包括拓扑构建、平衡、映射、势能推导和验证，将模拟时间从数十分钟缩短至 1 分钟，并在 27 个任务中有 22 个实现了与全原子模拟密度 5% 以内的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒化分子动力学可将聚合物模拟扩展到全原子方法无法触及的尺度，但自底向上的 CG 建模过程繁琐，且 CG 分辨率是设计选择，通用参数集通常不存在，需要为每种聚合物映射重新推导势能。

Method: 构建 CGMas 多智能体框架：LLM 推理智能体从聚合物名称推断全原子拓扑，分层自我纠正机制解决不饱和、含杂原子和极性聚合物常见的物理错误；下游智能体依次完成体系平衡、映射到 CG 表示、通过玻尔兹曼反演推导势能，并对标全原子参考体系进行基准测试。

Result: CGMas 完成了全部 27 个均聚物和共聚物任务，其中 22 个任务的密度与全原子结果误差在 5% 以内，模拟耗时从 38-88 分钟降至 1 分钟。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的大语言模型为实现聚合物粗粒化建模的全自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Coarse-grained (CG) molecular dynamics extends polymer simulation beyond the scales accessible to all-atom (AA) methods, but bottom-up CG modeling is laborious. The CG resolution is a design choice, so a transferable parameter set is generally not available and the potentials are derived anew for each polymer mapping. Here we present CGMas, a multi-agent framework that automates topology construction, equilibration, mapping, potential derivation, and validation from a natural-language specification of the polymer and target resolution. A large-language-model (LLM) reasoning agent infers the AA topology from polymer name, while layered self-correction resolves physical errors common to unsaturated, heteroatom-containing, and polar polymers. Downstream agents equilibrate the system, map it onto CG representation, derive potentials through Boltzmann inversion, and benchmark the model against its atomistic reference. CGMas completed all 27 homopolymer and copolymer tasks, matched the AA density to within 5% in 22, and reduced simulation from 38-88 min to 1 min, establishing agentic LLMs as a route to automated polymer coarse-graining.

</details>


### [73] [AgentPatch: Coarse-to-Fine Weak-Task Repair for Merging Agentic Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06699)
*Zibo Shao,Baochen Xiong,Chengdong Xu,Linhui Xiao,Kaichen Li,Haoran Gong,Yan Li,Yaguang Song,Xiaoshan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentPatch框架，解决Agentic MLLM合并中的非对称能力保留和行为关键遗忘问题，实现无需训练的粗到细修复，生成单一静态检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic MLLM模型针对特定工具或环境专用化，难以整合为单一通用模型。合并时面临两大挑战：非对称能力保留导致弱任务性能下降，行为关键遗忘导致长程执行偏离。

Method: 提出AgentPatch训练免疫粗到细修复框架：1) 选择稳定合并骨干网络；2) 通过弱任务唯一残差恢复恢复被稀释的弱任务特定信号；3) 应用智能体引导的行为关键补丁，在显式能力保护下恢复决定性行为。

Result: 在六个Agentic和多模态基准上实验表明，AgentPatch改进多种合并骨干网络，缓解弱任务退化，更好地平衡弱任务恢复与互补搜索和Agentic视觉处理能力的保留。

Conclusion: AgentPatch成功解决Agentic MLLM合并的核心挑战，提供了一种无需路由或集成的单一静态检查点方案，显著提升合并模型的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend multimodal perception and reasoning with planning, tool use, and interaction in dynamic environments. Yet current models are specialized for particular tools or environments, complicating consolidation into a single generalist. We formulate Agentic MLLM Merging and identify two challenges: asymmetric capability preservation, whereby capabilities with different interaction complexity are retained unevenly, producing weak tasks after merging, and behavior-critical forgetting, whereby losing decisive actions can derail long-horizon execution. We propose AgentPatch, a training-free coarse-to-fine repair framework. It selects a stable merged backbone, restores diluted weak-task-specific signals through Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery, and applies an Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch that recovers decisive behaviors under explicit capability protection. AgentPatch produces a single static checkpoint without routing or ensembles. Experiments across six agentic and multimodal benchmarks show that AgentPatch improves diverse merged backbones, alleviates weak-task degradation, and better balances weak-task recovery with the preservation of complementary search and agentic visual processing capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/ziboshao/AgentPatch.

</details>


### [74] [WebRider: Persona-Conditioned Intent Controllers for Live-Web Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06704)
*Zhi Li,Tao Zhou,Yeqing Li,Eugene Ie,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebRider 通过将委派策略形式化为意图契约，构建分层架构（契约维护、受保护可执行动作、工具层），并提出 RiderBench 基准（4096 个真实网页契约），实现对浏览路径的可审计、可人工判断、可学习的完整评估。实验显示强控制器虽完成率 99.2%，但仅 38.8% 符合所有策略约束；基于受保护中间接口训练的 8B 动作策略模型在固定控制器下超越仅执行基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前真实网页代理仅以最终答案评估，忽视委派中的策略约束（验证内容、不确定性处理、偏好、停止条件），导致看似正确的答案可能违背策略。全真审计发现强控制器完成率 99.2% 但仅 38.8% 遵守所有策略，完成不代表保真。

Method: 1. 将委派策略形式化为意图契约，包含目标、约束、证据义务、答案形式、任务级人设控制。2. 分层架构：顶层控制器维护契约，中层将意图实现为受保护的可执行动作，底层通过浏览器、搜索、地图工具执行。3. 构建 RiderBench 基准，覆盖 42 个公共网站的 4096 个真实网页契约，同时审计内部契约状态与可见用户体验。4. 利用受保护中间接口作为高质量训练信号，训练 8B 动作策略模型。

Result: 在 RiderBench 上，强控制器任务完成率 99.2%，但仅 38.8% 情况下遵守所有策略约束。基于受保护中间接口训练的 8B 动作策略模型在固定控制器下优于仅执行基线。系统使浏览路径成为一等对象，实现可审计、可人工判断、可学习，且不混淆动作实现与最终答案决策。

Conclusion: WebRider 通过意图契约与分层架构弥补了最终答案评估与策略保真之间的鸿沟，提供了可审计、可人工判断、可学习的真实网页代理评估框架，证明了将浏览路径作为一等对象的有效性。

Abstract: Delegating a web task involves more than asking a question; it requires transferring a policy: what to verify, how to handle uncertainty, which preferences matter, and when to stop. Yet, current live-web agents are evaluated solely on the final answer, ignoring the policy constraints that define the delegation. A plausible final answer can conceal violations of that policy. Our full live audit reveals this critical gap: a strong controller completes 99.2% of tasks but honors all policy constraints in only 38.8% of cases. Finishing does not imply fidelity. WebRider bridges this gap by formalizing the delegated policy as an intent contract---an operational record of goals, constraints, evidence obligations, answer form, and task-local persona controls that must hold even as web pages change. WebRider employs a hierarchical architecture: a top-layer controller maintains the contract, a middle layer realizes intentions as guarded executable actions, and a tool layer executes these actions via browser, search, and maps tools. Our benchmark, RiderBench, evaluates this design on 4,096 live-web contracts across 42 public websites, auditing both the internal contract state and the visible user experience to determine if a rollout preserved its policy and if the steps were persona-consistent. The guarded middle interface also serves as a high-quality training signal; an 8B action-policy model trained through this interface outperforms executable-only baselines under a fixed controller. By making the browsing path a first-class object, WebRider enables a system that is auditable, human-judgeable, and learnable without conflating action realization with final-answer decisions. Dataset URL: hf.co/datasets/WebRider/WebRider.

</details>


### [75] [MolBioKG: Grounding Out-of-Graph Molecules in Biomedical Knowledge Graphs via Multi-Resolution Structural Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06713)
*Yiming Zhang,Hikaru Shindo,Shuan Chen,Kaushalya Madhawa,Jun Jin Choong,Yuna Oikawa,Takashi Fujiwara,Keisuke Ozawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MolBioKG，一种双层系统，通过多层度结构锚定解决生物医学知识图谱中分子冷启动问题，无需任务特定训练即可处理未注册分子，并在多跳推理和图外泛化任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱管道假设查询分子已作为图实体存在，导致未注册分子无法接入图谱，形成冷启动挑战（即图外分子问题）。

Method: 构建包含 274 万分子的索引层（脚手架、片段、官能团、指纹）与 960 万边的知识图谱层。两种推理机制：静态多锚检索采用倒数排名融合（RRF），自适应遍历采用工具增强的 LLM 策略 Adapt-KG。仅需 SMILES 字符串即可检索结构相关实体并遍历生物医学邻域。

Result: 在图内链接恢复、复杂多跳推理、图外泛化三项任务上超越强基线。多跳推理 Hits@10 从 0.585 提升至 0.876，图外目标召回率从 0.145 提升至 0.269。预测保留可追踪的结构锚点和来源归属的图谱证据。

Conclusion: MolBioKG 有效解决了生物医学知识图谱中分子冷启动问题，通过多层度结构锚定和自适应 LLM 遍历，实现了无需任务特定训练的图外分子推理，显著提升了多跳推理和图外泛化性能，且保持了预测的可解释性和证据追溯性。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) accelerate drug discovery, but standard pipelines assume query molecules already exist as graph entities, leaving unregistered molecules disconnected. We address this cold-start challenge, termed the out-of-graph molecule problem, by introducing MolBioKG. This two-layer system grounds unseen molecules in biomedical evidence via multi-resolution structural anchoring. It connects an index of 2.74 million molecules (represented by scaffolds, fragments, functional groups, and fingerprints) to a 9.6-million-edge KG. Given only a SMILES string, MolBioKG retrieves structurally related graph entities and traverses their biomedical neighborhoods without task-specific training. It features two inference mechanisms: static multi-anchor retrieval using Reciprocal Rank Fusion, and Adapt-KG, a tool-using LLM policy for adaptive traversal. Evaluated across in-graph link recovery, complex multi-hop reasoning, and out-of-graph generalization, MolBioKG outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it raises Hits@10 from 0.585 to 0.876 in multi-hop reasoning and out-of-graph target recall from 0.145 to 0.269, all while ensuring predictions retain traceable structural anchors and source-attributed KG evidence.

</details>


### [76] [The Optimizer Is the Agent: Reasoning-Driven Search across Prompts, Programs, and ML Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06714)
*Junbo Li,Boyi Liu,Canwen Xu,Yite Wang,Yuxiong He,Zhangyang Wang,Qiang Liu,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReASearch，一个统一的推理驱动优化框架，让单一工具使用智能体内化搜索策略，自主决定评估、诊断、编辑和验证，跨提示词、程序和ML工作流优化，在14个任务上超越专用系统2%-40%，且涌现出复杂搜索行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词、程序和ML工作流优化系统依赖显式外循环控制器（进化搜索、多臂老虎机、文本梯度等），作者探讨能否将搜索策略内化到单一工具使用智能体中，实现自主推理驱动的优化。

Method: ReASearch采用统一智能体循环和领域特定工具，智能体自主决定评估内容、故障诊断、编辑操作、验证与重启时机，通过持久化记忆长期优化策略，同一脚手架适用于提示词、程序和ML工作流优化。

Result: 在14个多样化任务上，ReASearch与专用优化系统竞争且多为超越，相对强基线提升2%-40%，部分任务超越人类最佳已知结果；复杂搜索行为从智能体推理过程中自然涌现。

Conclusion: 推理驱动智能体可内化搜索策略，跨领域实现强性能优化，复杂搜索行为无需显式控制器即可涌现，展示了统一智能体框架在自动化优化中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent systems for optimizing prompts, programs, and ML workflows typically rely on explicit outer-loop controllers such as evolutionary search, bandits, or textual-gradient methods. We ask a fundamentally different question: how much of this search policy can be internalized by a single tool-using agent? We present ReASearch, a unified framework for reasoning-driven optimization in which the agent autonomously decides what to evaluate, how to diagnose failures, which edits to make, and when to verify or restart. Rather than serving only as a proposal generator guided by hand-designed heuristics, the agent actively analyzes outcomes, allocates budget, and refines its strategy over long horizons through persistent memory. With a shared agent loop and domain-specific tools, ReASearch instantiates the exact same scaffold to optimize prompts, programs, and ML workflows. Across 14 diverse tasks, it is competitive with and mostly better than specialized optimization systems, achieving gains of 2% to 40% over strong domain-specific baselines, and in some cases discovering solutions that improve on prior human best-known results. Crucially, we observe that complex search behaviors, which are typically implemented by explicit controllers, emerge naturally from the agent's reasoning process.

</details>


### [77] [bioMoR: Biology-Guided Mixture-of-Recursions for Effective Genomic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06727)
*Koushik Howlader,Tirtho Roy,Md Tauhidul Islam,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 bioMoR 框架，首次将 Mixture-of-Recursions (MoR) 应用于基因/通路级别的组学分析。通过三处整合结构化生物学知识（图嵌入优化、注意力结构偏置、图感知路由），在八个基准上显著超越无生物学先验的 MoR 基线，同时大幅降低参数量和计算量，并提供可解释的标记基因/通路及计算分配深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维组学数据包含数千基因/通路，但仅部分需要深度计算。现有 Transformer 模型对所有 token 统一处理，计算冗余大；MoR 通过自适应路由提升效率，但缺乏生物学先验指导。因此需要将结构化生物知识（如基因相互作用网络、通路拓扑）融入 MoR 骨干，指导 token 交互建模与计算深度分配。

Method: 提出 bioMoR，在 MoR 主干中三处注入生物学结构：1) 图嵌入层利用邻域信息细化 token 表示；2) 自注意力加入结构偏置，引导关注生物学相关 token；3) 图感知路由器根据邻域信息决定每个 token 的递归深度。模型在统一五折交叉验证下，针对八种组学数据类型进行基因/通路级分类任务。

Result: 八个基准平均 macro-F1 提升 8.2 个百分点，平衡准确率提升 7.1 个百分点，优于最强无生物学先验 MoR 基线。参数量减少 75%，FLOPs 最多降低 58%（对比非递归 Transformer）。所选标记基因/通路具有生物学可解释性，token 级递归深度揭示计算资源分配模式。

Conclusion: 结构化生物学知识能有效指导 MoR 中的 token 交互建模与自适应计算分配。bioMoR 实现了高精度、高效率与可解释性的统一，为高维组学分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transformer models for high-dimensional omics analysis process thousands of genes or pathways, although only a subset requires deep computation. Mixture-of-Recursions (MoR) improves efficiency through adaptive token-choice or expert-choice routing. We propose bioMoR, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first framework to apply MoR to gene-level and pathway-level learning. Our contributions include identifying three locations for integrating structured biological knowledge within an MoR backbone: graph-based information sharing refines token embeddings, a structural bias guides self-attention toward biologically related tokens, and a graph-aware router uses neighborhood information to determine each token's recursion depth. These techniques are centered on our insight that additional knowledge of token interaction can effectively help models construct embeddings and select which tokens should be learned more deeply. Across eight benchmarks spanning diverse omics data types and evaluated under a unified five-fold cross-validation protocol, bioMoR improves average macro-F1 by 8.2 percentage points and balanced accuracy by 7.1 percentage points over the strongest biology-agnostic MoR baseline while using 75 percent fewer parameters and up to 58 percent fewer FLOPs than a non-recursive Transformer. The selected marker genes or pathways provide biological interpretability, while their token-specific recursion depths reveal how computation is allocated.

</details>


### [78] [From Cheap Fakes to Pure Synthesis: Addressing the New Era of T2V Fake News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06732)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Liang Lan,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个纯合成假新闻视频数据集 PS-FNVD，将 T2V 假新闻视频检测建模为三元分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造），并设计了推理引导的 R-T2V 框架，结合高层语义逻辑与低层生成痕迹，实现了最优检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频生成模型可从零合成假新闻视频，超越了传统廉价伪造，现有数据集缺乏纯合成假新闻视频，直接用描述提示 T2V 模型生成样本会导致检测退化为单模态捷径并引发语义-视觉退化，因此需要新任务定义和数据集。

Method: 构建 PS-FNVD 数据集，包含两类纯合成假新闻：编造事件与对齐欺骗（类型 1）和真实事件与虚假视觉溯源（类型 2）；提出 R-T2V 框架，通过条件理由生成和监督微调训练，融合高层语义推理与低层物理生成痕迹进行三元真实性预测。

Result: 在 10 个主流基线上的大量实验表明，R-T2V 达到最先进性能，准确率比第二好基线高 12.20 个百分点，宏观 F1 高 8.46 个百分点。

Conclusion: 本文通过三元分类任务定义、首个纯合成假新闻视频数据集及推理引导检测框架，有效解决了 T2V 生成假新闻视频检测中的单模态捷径和语义-视觉退化问题，显著提升了检测性能。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) generation models enable fake news videos to be synthesized from scratch, shifting the threat beyond cheap fakes assembled from existing footage. Such news videos can closely match fabricated narratives, creating a modality alignment trap for existing detectors. Existing datasets lack pure synthesis fake news videos. Although directly prompting T2V models with descriptions of fake news videos can yield perfectly aligned samples, it reduces the fake news video detection (FNVD) to unimodal shortcuts and causes semantic-visual degeneration. To counter this, we formulate T2V-FNVD as a novel ternary classification task with three labels (real, cheap fake, and pure synthesis fake) and construct the first pure synthesis fake news video dataset (PS-FNVD). PS-FNVD includes fabricated events with aligned deception (Type 1) and true events with false visual provenance (Type 2), preventing models from exploiting unimodal shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the Reasoning-guided T2V-FNVD (R-T2V) framework. Trained through conditioned rationale generation and supervised fine-tuning, R-T2V integrates high-level semantic logic with low-level physical generative traces to predict the ternary veracity label. Extensive experiments across 10 prevailing baselines show that R-T2V achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the second-best baseline by 12.20 percentage points in accuracy and 8.46 percentage points in macro $F_1$.

</details>


### [79] [IB-RL: Isolated Bilateral Reinforcement Learning for Strategic Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06735)
*Senhao Wang,Chenghao Cai,Haitao Hu,Mingxing Huang,Xingguang Wang,Wenhao Li,Zecheng Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对强化学习在战略对话中面临的“静态对手错配”问题，提出隔离双边强化学习（IB-RL），让双方角色在联合轨迹中共同进化，同时保持各自独立的优势估计、动作掩码和更新路径，实现了对未见对手的更好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习在大语言模型上的应用多针对固定规则环境（如数学推理、代码执行），但在战略对话中环境是另一个会自适应策略的智能体。现有方法通常让目标智能体对抗固定对手或模拟器，导致策略利用特定对手的规律而非学习泛化策略，这就是静态对手错配问题。

Method: 提出隔离双边强化学习（IB-RL）：双方角色通过联合采样轨迹共同进化，但每个角色维护完全独立的优势函数、动作掩码和参数更新路径，互不干扰地优化各自奖励。

Result: 在 Vehicle TeleSales 任务上，IB-RL 达到 89.6% Success@1，优于最佳单边 RL 基线的 84.6%；在 Deal-or-NoDeal 谈判任务中，对抗 DeepSeek V4 Pro 达成协议率 98.4%，远超最佳单边基线的 86.4%。冻结策略在完全独立的保留对手上评估，验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过严格隔离的双边联合训练，可以训练出对未见对手泛化更有效的策略，解决了战略对话中静态对手错配的根本问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong results in improving large language models (LLMs) on tasks with stationary, verifiable rewards, such as mathematical reasoning and code execution. In these settings, the environment follows fixed rules and does not adapt strategically to the agent. Strategic dialogue differs in this respect: the environment is another agent that adapts to the policy, and success depends on the interaction between the two sides. Despite this interactive nature, current RL approaches typically train a target agent against a fixed counterpart or simulator. We find that this training paradigm encourages the policy to exploit counterpart-specific regularities rather than learn strategies that generalize across counterparts. We call this problem the static-counterpart mismatch, which we quantify directly in our experiments. To address it, we propose Isolated Bilateral Reinforcement Learning (IB-RL), in which the two roles coevolve through joint rollouts while each role optimizes its own reward through fully independent advantages, action masks, and update paths. We evaluate frozen policies against fully independent held-out counterparts in both domains. On Vehicle TeleSales, IB-RL achieves 89.6% Success@1, compared to 84.6% for the best unilateral RL baseline. On Deal-or-NoDeal, it reaches 98.4% agreement against DeepSeek V4 Pro, compared to 86.4% for the best unilateral baseline. These results indicate that jointly training both roles with strict peragent isolation produces policies that generalize more effectively to unseen counterparts.

</details>


### [80] [MemPrism: Task-Conditioned Relational Memory Views for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06745)
*Zhisheng Chen,Bingfan Zeng,Bangde Cao,Zhengwei Xie,Yuxuan Li,Jinhan Li,Zheng Lu,Xiangchen Guan,Zikai Xiao,Rui Qian,Jingwei Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemPrism 是一种任务条件关系记忆框架，将持久经验存储与决策时工作记忆分离，通过动态构建关系视图来解决长时程智能体中的表征不匹配问题，提升任务性能并降低内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统假设证据可通过固定表征直接消费，导致表征不匹配——相关信息虽存在但未针对当前决策组织。

Method: 提出 MemPrism，将交互记录为事件流，根据任务上下文动态构建关系视图。轻量级视图策略选择关系结构、证据范围、结果条件和粒度，确定性组合器和渲染器将历史事实转换为临时光学工作记忆视图供冻结任务策略使用。

Result: 在长时程具身和网页智能体基准上，MemPrism 持续提升任务性能，尤其在轨迹变长时，同时减少内存 token 消耗。学习的视图策略可跨不同 VLM 迁移无需额外适应。

Conclusion: 任务条件关系视图作为智能体的通用记忆接口是有效的。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents rely on memory to reuse experiences, yet existing memory systems often assume that evidence can be directly consumed through a fixed representation. This leads to representation mismatch, where relevant information is available but not organized for the current decision. To this end, we propose MemPrism, a task-conditioned relational memory framework that separates persistent experience storage from decision-time working memory. MemPrism records interactions as the event stream and dynamically constructs relational views according to the current task context. A lightweight view policy selects the relation structure, evidence range, outcome condition, and granularity, while a deterministic composer and render transform historical facts into a temporary optical working-memory view for a frozen task policy. Experiments on long-horizon embodied and web-agent benchmarks show that MemPrism consistently improves the task performance, especially as trajectories become longer, while reducing memory token consumption. Furthermore, the learned view policy transfers across different VLMs without additional adaptation, demonstrating the effectiveness of task-conditioned relational views as a general memory interface for agents.

</details>


### [81] [Mind the Gap: A Dual Knowledge Graph Framework for Unified Multi-task User Intent Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06752)
*Tzu-Cheng Peng,Chien Chin Chen,Chih-Hao Ku,Yung-Chun Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DKG-MTI，一种双知识图谱框架，用于从在线旅游评论中进行统一的多任务用户意图推理，通过动态构建用户特定意图知识图谱并与全球酒店知识图谱对齐，结合大语言模型同时预测方面评分和生成反向用户意图陈述，在 TripAdvisor 数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖层级流水线导致误差传播，或检索方法忽略领域知识中的结构关系，需要一种能够利用结构感知知识对齐的可扩展且可解释的意图推理方法。

Method: 提出推理时知识增强框架，动态从每条评论构建用户特定意图知识图谱，通过结构感知语义平滑与全球酒店知识图谱对齐，将对齐知识与原始评论结合输入大语言模型，同时预测方面评分和生成反向用户意图陈述。

Result: 在 TripAdvisor 评论实验中，DKG-MTI 在分类和意图生成任务上均持续优于强大的 LLM 和检索基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构感知知识对齐对于可扩展且可解释的意图推理有效，双知识图谱框架能够统一多任务用户意图推理。

Abstract: This paper proposes DKG-MTI, a dual knowledge graph framework for unified multi-task user intent inference from online travel reviews. Existing approaches often rely on hierarchical pipelines that suffer from error propagation or retrieval methods that ignore structural relationships in domain knowledge. To address these limitations, we introduce an inference-only knowledge augmentation framework that dynamically constructs a User-Specific Intent Knowledge Graph from each review and aligns it with a Global Hotel Knowledge Graph through structure-aware semantic smoothing. The aligned knowledge is combined with the original review and processed by a large language model to simultaneously predict aspect ratings and generate reverse user intent statements. Experiments on TripAdvisor reviews show that DKG-MTI consistently outperforms strong LLM and retrieval-based baselines in both classification and intent generation tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of structure-aware knowledge alignment for scalable and explainable intent inference.

</details>


### [82] [Capek 0.5: An Execution-Centric Vision-Language Model for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06756)
*Ying Chen,Weizhen Li,Zhe Hu,Zhenjiang Li,Rui Jiang,Zhifeng Gu,Lihuang Fang,Jiangping Liu,Lei Yi,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Capek 0.5 是一个以执行为中心的具身视觉语言模型，通过能力分类学将具身能力分为空间推理、时间理解、动作引导、状态验证四大类，先用强化学习训练专家模型，再通过权重空间合并和路由策略空间蒸馏融合为统一模型，在多个基准和闭环任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体方法通常针对孤立任务开发能力，缺乏围绕执行整体的组织与整合；需要一种以执行为中心的能力分类学，统一空间推理、时间理解、动作引导、状态验证等互补能力，且这些能力在监督信号、预测格式和验证标准上各不相同。

Method: 提出执行中心能力分类学，包含四大能力族；从共享骨干网络出发，通过可验证奖励的强化学习训练专家模型；再通过权重空间合并和路由策略空间蒸馏将专家融合为单一推理模型；在 2B 和 35B-A3B 规模实例化；在 Capek-StateBench 等基准、能力保持受控实验、闭环仿真环境中评估。

Result: 在大多数基准指标上优于初始化模型；在单一检查点中保留所有四大专业能力，并量化损失；成功迁移到闭环具身任务执行。

Conclusion: Capek 0.5 展示了基于执行中心分类学的具身能力整合路径，验证了专家训练与融合策略的有效性，为统一具身视觉语言模型提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly serving as the reasoning core of embodied agents. Robot execution is inherently iterative: each action reshapes the scene and physical state, continually renewing what must be perceived, reasoned about, and verified. Meeting these demands requires complementary capabilities that differ in supervision signals, prediction formats, and verification criteria. Existing approaches typically develop these capabilities against isolated, task-specific objectives, leaving open how they should be organized and integrated around execution as a whole. We present Capek 0.5, an embodied vision-language model built around an execution-centric capability taxonomy. Rather than organizing training by datasets or tasks, the taxonomy groups embodied capabilities according to their functional roles throughout execution and comprises four capability families: Spatial Reasoning, Temporal Understanding, Action Guidance, and State Verification. Each capability is first acquired by a dedicated specialist through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards from a shared backbone, and the specialists are then consolidated into a single inference-time model through weight-space merging followed by routed policy-space distillation. We instantiate Capek 0.5 at the 2B and 35B-A3B scales and evaluate it from three complementary perspectives: comprehensive benchmark suites including Capek-StateBench, a new benchmark for state verification; a controlled study of capability retention from specialists to the unified model; and closed-loop evaluation in simulated embodied environments. Capek 0.5 improves the large majority of matched benchmark rows over its initialization, retains all four specialized capabilities in one checkpoint with quantified losses, and transfers to closed-loop embodied task execution.

</details>


### [83] [LiFTER: A Grounded Neuro-Symbolic Microscope for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06765)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LiFTER，一种神经符号预测器，用于连续时间动态图（CTDG）的链路预测。它将观察到的交互作为基础时序事实保留，并应用可执行的时序规则进行预测，使预测过程可解释、可验证、可干预。在四个 CTDG 基准上，LiFTER 在历史负样本预测中表现有竞争力，并获得最高的宏观解释准确率和删除保真度。它还能作为显微镜，分离递归、历史位置、转移等因素的贡献，并追踪到单个事实。独立执行可重构所有 19,664 个测试预测的 logits，最大误差仅 0.0000131，实现了可验证的基础计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续时间动态图模型将过去交互压缩为神经状态进行预测，虽然预测有效，但掩盖了哪些实体跨事件共享以及时序模式如何贡献预测。作者将这一缺口视为预测架构的属性，而非事后解释问题，旨在构建一种保留观察到的交互作为基础事实、应用可执行时序规则、使预测证据和规则可检查、可独立重新计算、可干预的神经符号预测器。

Method: LiFTER 将观察到的交互作为基础时序事实保留，并应用可执行的时序规则对预查询事实进行推理。每个预测分数是规则执行的有符号求和，其历史事实、实体绑定和时序顺序被显式满足。这种神经符号架构使预测背后的证据和规则可被检查、独立重新计算和干预。模型在四个 CTDG 基准上进行训练和评估，同时作为显微镜分离递归、历史位置、转移等因素的贡献并追踪到单个事实。

Result: 在四个 CTDG 基准上，LiFTER 在历史负样本预测任务中达到有竞争力的性能，并获得最高的宏观解释准确率和删除保真度。同一架构还能作为显微镜，分离递归、历史位置、转移等因素在不同数据集上的贡献并追踪到单个事实。独立执行重构所有 19,664 个测试预测的 logits，最大误差仅为 0.0000131，验证了预测计算的可验证性。

Conclusion: LiFTER 将未来链路预测转化为可验证的基础计算，通过神经符号方法保留观察到的交互作为基础事实并应用可执行时序规则，实现了预测的可解释性、可验证性和可干预性，同时保持了有竞争力的预测性能。它为连续时间动态图建模提供了一个透明、可信的框架。

Abstract: Continuous-time dynamic graph models predict future links by compressing past interactions into neural states. Although effective for forecasting, this computation obscures which entities are shared across events and how temporal patterns contribute to a prediction. We treat this gap as a property of the predictive architecture rather than a problem to be addressed after prediction. Link-Fact Temporal Rule Inducer (LiFTER) is a neuro-symbolic predictor that preserves observed interactions as grounded temporal facts and applies executable tempo- ral rules to pre-query facts. Each score is a signed sum of rule exe- cutions whose historical facts, entity bindings, and temporal order are explicitly satisfied. The evidence and rules responsible for a prediction can therefore be inspected, independently recomputed, and intervened upon. Across four CTDG benchmarks, LiFTER achieves competitive historical-negative forecasting and the highest macro explanation ac- curacy and deletion fidelity. The same architecture also serves as a microscope that separates the contributions of recurrence, history po- sition, and transition across datasets and traces them to individual facts. Independent execution reconstructs all logits for 19,664 test predictions with a maximum error of 0.0000131. LiFTER turns future-link forecasting into a verifiable grounded computation.

</details>


### [84] [Surg-UniWorld: A Unified Surgical World Model with Multimodal Control Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06770)
*Rulin Zhou,Wanhao Liu,Guoheng Ma,Liangjin Shao,Qiujie Song,Yidu Wang,Guankun Wang,Tong Chen,Long Bai,Luping Zhou,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Surg-UniWorld，一种统一的手术世界模型，通过层级手术锚点和多模态控制专家解决异构视觉条件融合导致的解剖结构扭曲、器械外观漂移和时序不一致问题，并在Cholec80-SurgWAM基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控手术世界模型缺乏统一的多模态控制范式，直接融合异构视觉条件会导致解剖结构扭曲、器械外观漂移和时序不一致的交互，阻碍了手术AI和仿真的生成式基础建设。

Method: Surg-UniWorld包含三大核心组件：1) 层级手术锚点，利用首帧外观和层级语义掩码保持场景身份、解剖组织和交互边界；2) 锚点相对模态专家，相对共享锚点解读边缘、深度、光流证据，捕捉互补的边界、几何和运动信息；3) 多模态控制专家，执行贡献保留的分阶段组合激活的模态增量，为Wan2.2视频扩散骨干生成控制提示。同时构建Cholec80-SurgWAM基准支持多模态手术世界建模。

Result: 大量实验表明，Surg-UniWorld在生成质量、时序一致性和多模态可控性上持续优于现有的可控视频生成方法和手术世界模型基线。

Conclusion: Surg-UniWorld通过层级锚点和多模态控制专家实现了统一的手术世界建模，有效解决了异构条件融合的难题，为手术AI和仿真提供了高质量的生成式基础。

Abstract: Controllable surgical world models can provide a generative foundation for surgical artificial intelligence and simulation by synthesizing realistic instrument--tissue interactions. However, existing methods lack a unified multimodal control paradigm, while direct fusion of heterogeneous visual conditions often causes anatomical distortion, instrument appearance drift, and temporally inconsistent interactions. In this work, we propose {Surg-UniWorld}, a unified surgical world model with multimodal control experts. Surg-UniWorld first constructs a {Hierarchical Surgical Anchor} from first-frame appearance and hierarchical semantic masks to preserve persistent scene identity, anatomical organization, and interaction boundaries. {Anchor-Relative Modality Experts} then interpret edge, depth, and optical-flow evidence relative to the shared anchor, capturing complementary boundary, geometric, and motion information. A {Multimodal Control Expert} further performs contribution-preserving stage-wise composition of the activated modality increments and generates control hints for the Wan2.2 video diffusion backbone. To support multimodal surgical world modeling, we further construct Cholec80-SurgWAM, a benchmark for controllable surgical video generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Surg-UniWorld consistently outperforms existing controllable video generation methods and surgical world-model baselines in generation quality, temporal consistency, and multimodal controllability.

</details>


### [85] [Evolving Parallel Algorithm Portfolios via Potential-Aware Instance Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06808)
*Shaofeng Zhang,Shengcai Liu,Zhiyuan Wang,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PIAC框架解决LLM自动构建投资组合在组合优化问题上的泛化不足，引入无需参考解的潜在增益指标和LLM合成的多样化实例变异器，在TSP和CVRP上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM自动构建投资组合（LLM-ACP）在小样本场景下泛化能力差。实例与算法协同进化虽能通过生成困难实例增强泛化，但面临两大瓶颈：评估实例难度依赖高质量参考解，单一生成模式限制实例多样性。

Method: 提出PIAC框架，核心两点创新：1）潜在增益指标，通过扰动生成算法评估其在生成实例上的改进潜力，无需参考解即可估计泛化增益；2）利用LLM合成多样化实例变异器，探索更广泛的问题实例空间。框架在贪心构造、蚁群优化、引导局部搜索三种算法骨干上实例化。

Result: 在TSP和CVRP六种数据分布上的综合评估显示，PIAC持续优于最先进LLM-ACP基线，TSP贪心构造投资组合实现19.76%的相对提升。

Conclusion: PIAC通过无参考解的潜在增益度量和LLM驱动的多样化实例生成，有效解决了协同进化中的关键局限，显著提升了组合优化问题上算法投资组合的泛化性能。

Abstract: The Automatic Construction of Portfolios via Large Language Models (LLM-ACP) suffers from poor generalization in practical few-shot scenarios when solving complex combinatorial optimization problems. Instance and algorithm co-evolution frameworks address this by expanding the training dataset with generated hard instances on which the current algorithm portfolio underperforms, thereby enhancing generalization. However, this paradigm faces two critical limitations: evaluating instance hardness relies on high-quality reference solutions, and single-mode generation patterns limit instance diversity. To overcome these limitations, we introduce the Potential-aware Instance and Algorithm Co-evolution (PIAC) framework. Our core contribution is twofold. First, we propose potential gain, a novel metric that eliminates the need for reference solutions. This metric estimates generalization gain by perturbing the generated algorithms and assessing their improvement potential on generated problem instances. Second, PIAC leverages LLMs to synthesize diverse instance mutators, exploring a broader region of the problem-instance space and thereby enhancing the portfolio's generalization capabilities. Given that perturbation spaces vary across different algorithms, we instantiate our framework on Greedy Constructive, Ant Colony Optimization, and Guided Local Search algorithmic backbones. Comprehensive evaluations on the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) and Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) across six distinct data distributions demonstrate that PIAC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-ACP baselines, notably achieving a 19.76% relative improvement for TSP Greedy Constructive portfolios.

</details>


### [86] [Gated-BEPO: Confidence-Gated Bellman Credit Assignment for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06861)
*Hongxi Yan,Ziyue Huang,Shichao Fan,Qingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gated-BEPO 方法，用于在长时程环境中训练大语言模型智能体，通过经验回放图估计状态值并结合置信门控自适应融合回合级与步级信用分配，在 WebShop、ALFWorld 和视觉 Sokoban 任务上取得持续性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无评论家方法将轨迹级奖励均匀传播到各步骤，无法区分失败轨迹中的有用动作与成功轨迹中的无效动作；近期基于状态匹配的分组方法虽构建步级信用，但直接依赖单条轨迹结果且采用固定权重融合回合级信用，缺乏自适应性。

Method: Gated-BEPO 为每个回放组构建经验图，利用均值备份贝尔曼不动点估计节点值（反映当前策略的经验动作分布），再沿采样轨迹累积时序差分残差（广义优势估计），得到同时捕捉即时与下游效应的步级贝尔曼优势；引入置信门控：仅在观测到多个后继状态的状态引入贝尔曼信用，否则回退至回合级信用。

Result: 在 WebShop、ALFWorld 和视觉 Sokoban 三个基准上，Gated-BEPO 在语言模型与视觉语言模型上均表现出稳定的性能提升；消融实验验证了贝尔曼不动点价值估计的有效性，并表明步级信用应选择性而非均匀地融入最终优势。

Conclusion: Gated-BEPO 通过经验图上的贝尔曼价值估计与置信门控自适应融合，解决了长时程稀疏奖励下的信用分配难题，实现了步级信用的精准且鲁棒的利用，为大模型智能体在复杂环境中的训练提供了更有效的范式。

Abstract: Training large language model agents in long-horizon environments requires assigning credit from sparse terminal outcomes to individual actions. Existing critic-free methods propagate trajectory-level rewards uniformly across steps, while recent approaches construct step-level groups by matching repeated states and compare actions within each group. The former cannot distinguish useful actions in failed trajectories from ineffective actions in successful ones. The latter rely on step credit derived directly from individual trajectory outcomes and fixed-weight fusion with episode-level credit. We propose Gated-BEPO, which derives step-level credit from empirical rollout graphs. For each rollout group, Gated-BEPO constructs an empirical graph and estimates node values through a mean-backup Bellman fixed point that reflects the empirical action distribution of the current policy. We then accumulate these temporal-difference residuals along each sampled trajectory using generalized advantage estimation, yielding step-level Bellman advantages that capture both immediate and downstream effects. To adaptively fuse episode- and step-level credit, a confidence gate incorporates Bellman credit only at states with multiple observed successors and otherwise uses episode-level credit. Experiments on WebShop, ALFWorld, and visual Sokoban show consistent improvements across language and vision-language models, while diagnostic ablations support the effectiveness of Bellman fixed-point value estimation and show that step-level credit should be incorporated selectively rather than uniformly into the final advantage.

</details>


### [87] [CEDAR: Agent-Orchestrated Tree Search for Goal-Directed Optimization of Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06871)
*Yingtao Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CEDAR利用LLM驱动的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）自动发现满足用户指定行为目标的复杂系统，通过LLM评判器作为适应度函数、LLM编辑器作为变异算子，实现目标导向的复杂系统设计，显著降低人工建模成本并促进跨领域应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统建模面临两大核心挑战：一是从反馈结构预测涌现行为极其困难，使得目标导向设计成为人工生命领域的核心开放问题；二是现有建模语言（如DYNAMO、STELLA）工作流繁琐劳动密集，限制了采用和及时决策。

Method: 提出CEDAR框架：将复杂系统表示为受限的可运行Python子集并引入领域特定原语；采用LLM驱动的MCTS变体，LLM Judge评估涌现行为与目标的契合度（价值函数），LLM Editor提出改进变体（转移核），形成生成-评估循环；该方法保持解的多样性并提供LLM级可解释性，揭示结构变化如何驱动涌现行为。

Result: CEDAR在减少人工投入的同时，实现了现有方法难以达成的能力，能够自动发现满足行为目标的复杂系统，推动复杂系统建模在更多领域的广泛采纳。

Conclusion: CEDAR作为一种自主的复杂系统发现方法，通过LLM与MCTS的深度耦合解决了目标导向设计中的预测难题和建模效率瓶颈，为人工生命及跨学科复杂系统应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Complex systems, core objects of study in artificial life, model diverse phenomena through nonlinear, feedback-driven interactions that produce emergent behavior, with applications from population dynamics and biology to economic policy and strategic decision-making. Yet the difficulty of predicting how feedback structure gives rise to emergent behavior, a central open problem in artificial life, makes goal-directed design exceptionally challenging. In established practice, system structures are written in specialized modeling languages such as DYNAMO or STELLA, compounding the challenge with labor-intensive workflows that limit adoption and hinder timely decision-making. To address these challenges, we introduce CEDAR, an autonomous method that uses Large Language Model (LLM) agents to discover complex systems satisfying user-specified behavioral goals. Our key innovation is an LLM-driven Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) deeply coupled with complex systems: at each iteration, an LLM Judge evaluates emergent behavior against specified goals and an LLM Editor proposes improved variants, with the Judge acting as a fitness function and the Editor as a variation operator, akin to a generate-and-evaluate loop in evolutionary computation. We represent complex systems as a restricted, runnable subset of Python with domain-specific primitives, letting LLMs modify system dynamics directly. CEDAR formalizes this as an MCTS variant with an LLM-parameterized transition kernel and value function, enabling goal-directed discovery of complex system behaviors while preserving solution diversity, and its LLM-based interpretability reveals how structural changes drive emergent behavior. CEDAR reduces human effort while enabling capabilities difficult to achieve with existing approaches, facilitating broader adoption of complex systems across domains.

</details>


### [88] [SkillEval: Decomposing Agent Skill Quality into Interpretable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06891)
*Jiahui Han,Qinuo Li,Ziheng Peng,Haotian Wu,Haoze Liu,Danfeng Shan,Guanchu Wang,Huiqi Deng,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SkillEval，一种可解释的文档级技能评估框架，通过学习隐藏表示空间中的方向来评估技能文档的各项质量属性，并能指导针对性修订以提升下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能评估主要依赖下游任务表现，只能反映技能与特定任务的兼容性，无法全面评估技能质量，也无法指出需改进的具体方面。研究发现SKILL.md文档的通用属性对技能质量至关重要，因此需要一种可解释的文档级评估方法。

Method: SkillEval从受控的正负技能对中学习每个质量属性在模型隐藏表示空间中的可解释方向，通过将新技能的表示投影到这些固定方向上进行评分；同时测量并减少文档长度、格式等无关特征的影响，使得分更具体地捕捉语义属性。

Result: 在受控质量测试中，SkillEval能可靠区分不同质量的技能；其评分与下游任务表现高度相关，可作为技能是否有助于任务完成的早期指标；利用SkillEval诊断弱点并指导针对性修订后，修订后的技能在目标属性上得到提升，并在下游任务中获得更高通过率。

Conclusion: SkillEval提供了一种可解释、文档级的技能评估框架，不仅能全面评估技能质量，还能指导技能文档的针对性改进，从而提升智能体在下游任务中的表现。

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable procedural knowledge that helps agents solve specialized tasks. As their use expands, evaluating skill quality becomes increasingly important. Existing evaluations often measure skill quality by testing whether a skill improves performance on specific downstream tasks. However, a reusable skill may apply to multiple task scenarios. Downstream evaluation mainly reflects the compatibility between a skill and the evaluated task, provides only a partial view of skill quality, and does not identify which aspect of the skill should be improved. We find that general properties of the \texttt{SKILL.md} document play an important role in skill quality. To evaluate these properties, we propose \textbf{SkillEval}, an interpretable framework for document-level skill evaluation. SkillEval evaluates each property using a fixed and inspectable scoring direction, producing interpretable scores. It further measures and reduces the influence of unrelated document features, such as length and formatting, so that each score captures its intended semantic property more specifically. Specifically, SkillEval learns an interpretable direction for each quality property from controlled positive--negative skill pairs in the hidden representation space of the model, and scores a new skill by projecting its representation onto these fixed directions. We use SkillEval to evaluate skills in controlled quality tests and show that SkillEval reliably distinguishes skills of different quality. In addition, SkillEval scores closely reflect downstream task performance, providing an early indication of whether a skill is likely to help an agent complete a task. We further explore SkillEval for diagnosing weaknesses in skill documents and guiding targeted revisions. The revised skills improve the targeted properties and achieve higher pass rates on downstream tasks.

</details>


### [89] [From Points to Edges: Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operators for Physics-Sensitive PDE Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06894)
*Zhentao Tan,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operator (ESO)，通过Pairwise-Variation Modal Mixer (PVMM)将局部边信息注入频谱模态混合，并引入Physics-Aware Reweighting (PAR)强调物理重要区域，在九个PDE基准上达到SOTA性能，显著降低系数跳变、高梯度流结构等物理敏感区域的求解误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱算子主要基于中心点表示进行模态混合，难以响应PDE中的物理敏感局部结构（如Darcy流中的材料界面导致的渗透率剧烈变化），导致在局部结构变化剧烈区域的逼近能力不足。

Method: 1. 提出Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operator (ESO)框架，利用Pairwise-Variation Modal Mixer (PVMM)将局部边向变化信息注入频谱模态选择，实现全局谱混合对局部结构的自适应调制。2. 引入任务自适应的Physics-Aware Reweighting (PAR)，基于任务特定物理量识别并加权物理重要区域。

Result: 在九个PDE基准测试中，ESO持续达到最先进性能。可视化和区域误差分析表明，ESO在系数跳变、高梯度流结构等物理敏感区域显著降低求解误差。

Conclusion: ESO在保持谱神经算子全局逼近能力的同时，通过边条件调制和物理感知重加权，有效捕捉物理敏感局部结构，为求解含复杂局部物理结构的PDE提供了更鲁棒的算子学习框架。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a central tool for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), with spectral operators offering efficient global mixing across spatial locations. However, many PDEs contain physics-sensitive local structures that are critical to the underlying physical behavior. For example, in Darcy flow, local material interfaces are often reflected by sharp changes in the permeability field and can strongly influence the solution. Existing spectral operators primarily adapt modal mixing based on center-point representations, making them insufficiently responsive to such localized structural variations. We propose the Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operator (ESO), a novel spectral operator framework that modulates global spectral mixing using local edge-wise variations. By incorporating the Pairwise-Variation Modal Mixer (PVMM) to inject local edge information into spectral mode selection, ESO preserves the global approximation capability of spectral neural operators while enabling the learned kernel to adapt to physics-sensitive local structures. Furthermore, we introduce a task-adaptive Physics-Aware Reweighting (PAR) that emphasizes physically important regions, identified by taskspecific physical quantities. Across nine PDE benchmarks, ESO consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Visual and region-wise analyses further demonstrate that ESO reduces solution errors near coefficient jumps, high-gradient flow structures, and other physically sensitive regions. The code is available at https://github.com/Tanpig-X/ESO.

</details>


### [90] [Long-Horizon Agent Trajectory Attribution: A Unified Benchmark and Fine-Grained Annotation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06909)
*Jing Chen,Yang Sun,Li Zhang,Lin Xu,Jie Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了轨迹归因任务，构建了统一的基准和标注框架，用于细粒度分析LLM智能体长程轨迹中的归因组件，包含1300+标注轨迹，定义了两个评估任务并提供基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估行为结果，缺乏对LLM智能体长程轨迹中细粒度归因分析的支持，需要统一的框架来标注和评估轨迹中的主要归因组件、攻击链和执行链。

Method: 构建统一组件模式组织异构轨迹，标注主要归因组件及攻击/执行链；基于AgentDojo和Agent3Sigma实例化基准，得到1300+标注轨迹；定义主要归因定位和归因链恢复两个评估任务；提供基于增量轨迹贡献和组件级留一消融的基线方法。

Result: 基准涵盖任务对齐动作、不安全动作和安全拒绝等多样场景，捕捉局部和长程归因及结构化归因链；基线结果在不同设置下表现差异显著，揭示了归因任务的挑战；发布了可复用的标注技能支持新模型轨迹标准化评估。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个轨迹归因基准和标注框架，为LLM智能体行为的细粒度归因分析提供了统一标准和评估工具，推动了智能体安全性和可解释性研究。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate through long-horizon trajectories involving user instructions, tool use, external observations, and memory. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate behavioral outcomes but provide limited support for fine-grained attribution analysis. We introduce trajectory attribution and develop a benchmark and annotation framework for this task. The benchmark organizes heterogeneous trajectories under a unified component schema and provides annotations of the primary attribution component, together with attack and execution chains where applicable. Instantiating the benchmark with trajectories from AgentDojo and the Stage and Canary settings of Agent3Sigma yields more than 1,300 annotated trajectories covering task-aligned actions, unsafe actions, and safety refusals. The benchmark defines two evaluation tasks, primary attribution localization and attribution-chain recovery, and provides reference baselines based on incremental trajectory contribution and component-level leave-one-out perturbation. It captures diverse attribution settings, including local and long-range attribution as well as structured attribution chains. Reference baseline results exhibit substantial performance differences across these settings, providing an initial characterization of the benchmark's attribution challenges. Beyond this initial instantiation, we release a reusable annotation skill that enables trajectories generated by new agent models to be standardized, annotated, and evaluated under the same framework. Project resources and future releases are available at https://github.com/chenjing-2024/agent-trajectory-attribution.

</details>


### [91] [Fast LapSum: Exact Differentiable Top-k at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06912)
*Łukasz Struski,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Jakub Antczak,Marcin Mazur,Kamil Książek,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fast LapSum，一种精确预算的软 Top-k 原语，在排序后线性时间内运行，保持可微分性且开销极低，使得大规模稀疏计算中的精确软 Top-k 成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准硬 Top-k 阻断梯度，现有软松弛方法计算代价过高，难以应用于大规模模型。需要一种既能保持精确选择质量（总质量为 k）又能全程可微分、且计算效率极高的 Top-k 原语。

Method: Fast LapSum 结合线性时间阈值计算与解析向量-雅可比积；针对极大规模采用概率括定策略，仅对核噪声分数中不确定的中间带进行排序。该方法在保持精确选择质量为 k 的前提下实现了端到端可微分。

Result: 求解器处理 10^6、10^7、10^8 个分数分别仅需 0.41、1.15、5.23 ms，开销近乎可忽略。在百万像素稀疏对抗样本生成（预算约 0.02% 像素）和从头训练全可微稀疏图像编码器两个高要求任务中，实现了数量级加速。

Conclusion: Fast LapSum 通过创新的线性时间算法和概率加速技术，解决了大规模稀疏计算中精确软 Top-k 的效率瓶颈，为稀疏路由、检索和大规模优化提供了实用的可微分基元。

Abstract: The top-$k$ operation is a fundamental building block of modern sparse computation, enabling token routing, expert activation, memory selection, and attention pruning. Yet standard hard top-$k$ blocks gradients, while existing continuous (soft) relaxations remain too costly for large-scale models. We introduce Fast LapSum, an exact-budget soft top-$k$ primitive whose GPU solver runs in linear time after sorting. Unlike prior linear-time methods such as DFTopK, which relax the normalization constraint, Fast LapSum is, to our knowledge, the first method to preserve an exact selection mass of $k$ while remaining fully differentiable end-to-end. Our solver combines a linear-time threshold computation with an analytical vector--Jacobian product, and for extreme scales employs probabilistic bracketing to sort only the uncertain middle band of kernel-noised scores. The resulting overhead is almost negligible: the solver processes $10^6$, $10^7$, and $10^8$ scores in $0.41$, $1.15$, and $5.23$\,ms, respectively. This makes exact soft top-$k$ practical for sparse routing, retrieval, and large-scale optimization. We demonstrate Fast LapSum on two demanding applications operating over millions of coordinates inside the training loop: generating megapixel sparse adversarial examples with an exact soft budget of ${\sim}0.02\%$ of an image's pixels, achieving an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods, and training a fully differentiable sparse image coder from scratch.

</details>


### [92] [ReGraph: Learning to Generate Recipe Graphs from Food Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06917)
*Guoshan Liu,Bin Zhu,Pengkun Jiao,Jingjing Chen,Chong-Wah Ngo,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReGraph 数据集和 Recipe Graph Learning (RGL) 两阶段框架，解决大型多模态模型（LMM）从食物图像生成食谱时缺乏结构化过程知识的问题。实验显示现有方法文本生成分数高但结构对齐差，RGL 显著提升实体与关系生成，但细粒度配料状态捕捉仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烹饪是结构化变换过程，配料经有序动作发生状态变化，但自由文本食谱将实体、中间状态和依赖隐含且纠缠。图表示可显式化程序知识，为评估模型是否编码过程级知识而非仅生成合理文本提供结构化基础。

Method: 1. 构建 ReGraph 大规模食谱图数据集：将配料、烹饪动作、工具建模为实体，用实体属性描述配料状态变化，用类型化关系编码操作目标、目的地和程序顺序；并引入显式食谱推理链式思维（RR-CoT）轨迹作为辅助监督。2. 提出 Recipe Graph Learning (RGL) 两阶段框架，使 LMM 能从食物图像生成细粒度烹饪工作流的结构化食谱图。

Result: 在确定性、模式感知匹配协议下，现有方法虽取得有竞争力的文本生成分数，但在 ReGraph 模式下仅能恢复有限的参考对齐实体和关系结构。RGL 在两种代表性 LMM 骨干上均持续提升烹饪实体和程序关系的生成；分析进一步表明细粒度配料状态捕捉仍是最具挑战的维度。

Conclusion: 图表示使程序知识显式化和组合化；RGL 框架有效提升 LMM 生成结构化食谱图的能力；但细粒度配料状态变化的捕捉仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Recent Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved impressive performance in recipe generation from food images.However, cooking is a structured transformation process in which ingredients undergo state changes through ordered actions,while free-form recipe language leaves the corresponding entities, intermediate states, and dependencies largely implicit and entangled.A graph representation makes this procedural knowledge explicit and compositional, providing a structured basis for assessing whether model outputs encode process-level knowledge rather than merely presenting plausible textual descriptions. To address this limitation, we present ReGraph, a large-scale recipe graph dataset that represents ingredients, cooking actions, and tools as entities, uses entity attributes to describe ingredient state changes, and employs typed relations to encode manipulation targets, destinations, and procedural ordering. ReGraph further incorporates explicit Recipe Reasoning Chain-of-Thought (RR-CoT) traces, providing auxiliary supervision for procedural decomposition and structured graph generation.
