*Yuehao Huang,Yunzi Wu,Xiaotao Zhang,Xinhai Li,Jiankun Dong,Jiajun Lv,Chi Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Yong Liu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WNM-3D，一种带有3D场景条件的生成式世界导航模型，通过几何感知表示和块因果注意力提升连续视觉语言导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的VLA策略虽具语义能力，但未显式建模智能体视觉观测随预测动作的演变；现有生成式世界动作模型（WAM）在连续VLN中未基于历史观测推断的几何感知表示条件化联合未来视图和动作生成。

Method: 使用冻结的前馈几何编码器从单目自我中心RGB历史提取几何感知表示，配合可训练的3D Scene-to-Token Adapter转换为固定长度前缀，通过块因果注意力条件化扩散Transformer中的每个未来视频-动作块。训练采用A*生成演示的监督世界动作微调、DAgger风格的策略访问状态适应、以及基于DanceGRPO的闭环策略优化。

Result: 在GN-Bench上，WNM-3D在闭环导航中优于强VLM导航策略及其2D条件对应模型；在固定近目标评估集上，实现更高的流动作一致性和更低的视觉运动误差。

Conclusion: WNM-3D通过引入3D场景条件和几何感知表示，有效整合历史观测为持久场景上下文，显著提升连续VLN的导航性能和动作-视觉一致性。

Abstract: Recent vision-language navigation (VLN) systems increasingly adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into vision-language-action (VLA) policies that map egocentric observations and language instructions directly to navigation actions. Although semantically capable, such action-centric training does not explicitly model how the agent's visual observations should evolve under its predicted motion. Generative world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, yet existing WAMs for continuous VLN do not condition joint future-view and action generation on geometry-aware representations inferred from the observed history. We present WNM-3D, a generative World Navigation Model with 3D scene conditioning for continuous VLN. To consolidate past observations into persistent scene context, a frozen feed-forward geometry encoder extracts geometry-aware representations from the monocular egocentric RGB history, and a trainable 3D Scene-to-Token Adapter converts them into a fixed-length prefix in the token space of the world-action Diffusion Transformer. Through block-causal attention, this prefix conditions every future video-action block, providing a shared geometric context for both future-view and action generation. We train WNM-3D through supervised world-action fine-tuning on A*-generated demonstrations, DAgger-style adaptation on policy-visited states, and DanceGRPO-based closed-loop policy optimization. Experiments on GN-Bench show that WNM-3D outperforms strong VLM-based navigation policies and its 2D-conditioned counterpart in closed-loop navigation. On a fixed near-goal evaluation set, WNM-3D also achieves higher flow-action consistency and lower visual-motion error.

</details>


### [128] [Winning by Peeking: Unenforced Budgets and Test-Set Selection Inflate Short-Budget AutoML Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07303)
*Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过案例研究揭示了短时间预算（几十秒）下 AutoML 系统对比常因协议缺陷（测试集选择偏差和预算未强制执行）导致误导性结果；修正后优势消失，并提供公平对比清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时间预算下的 AutoML 系统对比常见于工具 README 和工作坊论文，但容易出错。本文旨在通过实证案例研究，展示协议缺陷如何导致误导性的胜率报告，并量化选择偏差与计算资源不均的影响。

Method: 在 513 个 OpenML 数据集上对比 Orcetra、FLAML 和 AutoGluon，初始协议存在两个缺陷：(1) 搜索循环在测试集上为每个候选模型评分并报告最优，导致指标成为数十个噪声估计的最大值，而基线仅在训练数据选择、仅触碰一次测试集；(2) 预算仅在启动候选前检查，运行期间不强制执行，导致实际中位耗时 120 s，远超名义 60 s。随后修正协议：选择移至验证集、外部强制截止、所有框架均分计算资源，并重新运行。

Result: 修正后，Orcetra 在重跑子集上的胜率从 59.4% 跌至 34.3%，与任一竞争对手的成对差异均不再显著。选择规则贡献了 4.8 个百分点的下降，其余主要源于计算资源不均。同一轨迹数据还给出了随预算变化的选择偏差实测值：随候选数 K 增长但仅达 0.27 准确率点，约为 σ√(2ln K) 理论界的五分之一，因为共享测试行使大部分噪声相互抵消。

Conclusion: 短预算 AutoML 对比极易受协议缺陷影响；修正选择偏差与预算执行后，原本显著的优势消失。论文最后提供了一个用于短预算公平对比的检查清单，并开源了代码、逐数据集结果及复现脚本。

Abstract: Comparisons between AutoML systems at short time budgets -- tens of seconds rather than hours -- are common in tool READMEs and workshop papers, and they are easy to get wrong. We report a case study in which a simple AutoML engine, Orcetra, appeared to beat FLAML and AutoGluon on 513 OpenML datasets, winning 57.1% of them at a nominal 60-second budget and 78.4% of datasets against FLAML alone at 30 seconds. Both margins came from protocol defects that a results table cannot show. The search loop scored every candidate on the test split and reported the best, making the headline metric a maximum over dozens of noisy estimates while the baselines selected on training data and touched the test set once; and the budget was checked before launching a candidate but never enforced during one, so the system consumed a median of 120 s against a 60-second budget, 2.24x the wall-clock AutoGluon used. Re-running with selection moved to a validation split, the deadline enforced externally and every framework pinned to an equal share of the machine, Orcetra's win rate on the re-run subset falls from 59.4% to 34.3% and no pairwise difference against either competitor remains significant. Recording both estimands inside a single search lets us attribute the collapse: the selection rule accounts for 4.8 percentage points and unequal compute for most of the rest. The same traces give the selection bias as a function of budget, measured rather than assumed: it grows with $K$ but reaches only 0.27 accuracy points, about five times below the $σ\sqrt{2\ln K}$ bound a marginal-standard-error argument predicts, because candidates scored on shared test rows cancel most of the noise. We close with a checklist for short-budget comparisons. Code, per-dataset results and the scripts that regenerate every number and figure in the paper are released with it.

</details>


### [129] [An End-to-End Agent Auditing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07346)
*Haoning Wang,Mingxun Zhang,Chenyue Yu,Yingjun Shang,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A^2E（Agent Auditing Engine），一个面向Agent Harness的端到端评估引擎，基于Agent Task Protocol (ATP)实现任务快速集成，通过自动化Monitor采集标准化执行轨迹，并使用多维指标（执行效率、工具使用、任务规划、错误恢复）系统评估Harness能力。实验表明不同模型-Harness组合在不同任务类型上表现差异大，无单一组合全优，凸显系统化评估的必要性，为模型与Harness的协同演进提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）快速发展，Harness成为部署Agent的核心基础设施，但缺乏高效、系统、全面的端到端评估管线，难以细粒度刻画不同Harness在执行效率、工具使用、任务规划、错误恢复等维度的能力差异，且模型与Harness组合的性能随任务类型变化大，亟需系统化评估框架。

Method: 提出A^2E评估引擎，包含三大核心组件：1) Agent Task Protocol (ATP)：统一任务接口，支持快速集成异构Harness；2) 自动化插桩Monitor：运行时自动捕获并生成标准化执行轨迹；3) 多维评估指标体系：超越单一正确率，从执行效率、工具使用、任务规划、错误恢复四个维度量化Harness能力。

Result: 在多个Harness与模型组合上的实验显示：不同组合在不同任务类型上性能差异显著，不存在单一组合在所有任务上均优；多维指标能揭示正确率掩盖的细粒度能力差异（如工具调用效率、规划质量、错误恢复能力），验证了系统化评估的必要性。

Conclusion: A^2E提供了首个面向Agent Harness的端到端系统化评估引擎，通过ATP协议、自动化监控与多维指标，实现了Harness能力的细粒度刻画。实验结论强调模型与Harness需针对具体任务场景协同优化，而非追求单一最优组合，为Agent生态的协同演进提供了实证依据与工具支撑。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), harnesses have become essential infrastructure for deploying agents across a wide range of domains. The fast-evolving harness ecosystem has also made rigorous capability evaluation increasingly important. However, efficiently building an end-to-end, systematic, and comprehensive evaluation pipeline remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we introduce $A^2E$ (Agent Auditing Engine), an end-to-end evaluation engine designed for agent harnesses. $A^2E$ leverages our newly proposed Agent Task Protocol (ATP) to enable the rapid integration of evaluation tasks with different harnesses. Through an automatically instrumented Monitor, it captures and generates standardized execution traces during experiments. In the Evaluation stage, $A^2E$ systematically assesses harness capabilities using a suite of multidimensional metrics. Compared with correctness alone, these metrics provide a more fine-grained characterization of differences among harnesses in execution efficiency, tool use, task planning, and error recovery. Experiments conducted with $A^2E$ further reveal that model-harness combinations exhibit substantial performance variation across different types of tasks, and that no single combination consistently outperforms all others across every task. These findings not only demonstrate the necessity of systematic evaluation but also provide useful guidance for the co-evolving of models and harnesses. Our code is available at https://github.com/datamllab/A2E.

</details>


### [130] [QFCQT: A Chaotically Gated Quantformer Framework for Volatile Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07363)
*Junkai Lin,Siqi Hou,Raymond Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为QFCQT（量子分形灵感混沌门控Quantformer）的模型，用于非平稳时间序列预测，通过引入可学习的Lee振子激活模块和平滑-混沌门控融合机制，有效捕捉长程依赖、局部波动爆发和非线性振荡行为，在多个基准上优于现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的预测器虽能建模长程依赖，但其前馈块通常依赖平滑静态激活函数，对突发的机制变化不够敏感，难以应对非平稳时间序列中的长程依赖、局部波动爆发、结构性偏移和非线性振荡行为。

Method: 提出QFCQT模型，包含三大核心组件：(1) Quantformer风格的数值编码器，通过线性嵌入直接处理多变量输入；(2) 可学习的Lee振子激活模块，将标量预激活映射为动态振荡响应，并通过时间维度最大池化汇总；(3) 平滑-混沌门控融合机制，自适应平衡传统平滑激活与混沌敏感响应。此外，采用八个参数化Lee振子族的软叠加，自适应捕捉不同机制下的非线性响应模式。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股股指基准上的实验表明，QFCQT持续优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等强基线模型。

Conclusion: QFCQT通过融合振子启发的非线性激活与混沌门控机制，有效解决了非平稳时间序列预测中的复杂动态建模难题，为金融等领域的波动预测提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Forecasting non-stationary time series remains difficult due to long-range dependencies, local volatility bursts, structural shifts, and nonlinear oscillatory behaviors. Although Transformer-based forecasters are effective for modeling long-term temporal dependencies, their feed-forward blocks typically rely on smooth static activations that are insufficiently sensitive to abrupt regime changes. Motivated by quantitative Transformer designs and oscillator-based nonlinear activations, we propose QFCQT, short for Quantum-Fractal-inspired Chaotically Gated Quantformer, for robust forecasting under complex volatile dynamics. Here, "quantum-fractal-inspired" denotes a computational analogy based on soft oscillator superposition and multi-scale nonlinear responses, rather than a formal quantum-mechanical or fractal-theoretic derivation. QFCQT consists of three main components: (1) a Quantformer-style numerical encoder that directly processes multivariate inputs via linear embedding; (2) a learnable Lee-oscillator activation module that maps scalar pre-activations to dynamic oscillatory responses and summarizes them through Max-over-Time pooling; and (3) a smooth-chaotic gated fusion mechanism that adaptively balances conventional smooth activations and chaos-sensitive responses. Furthermore, instead of using a single fixed oscillator, QFCQT employs a soft superposition of eight parameterized Lee oscillator families to adaptively capture different nonlinear response patterns across regimes. Experiments on ETTh1, ETTh2, and A-share Stock Index benchmarks show that QFCQT consistently outperforms strong baselines, including Informer, LogTrans, LSTMa, HAT, and COTN.

</details>


### [131] [Curriculum as Code: An AI-Assisted Architecture for Instructional Design in STEM Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07364)
*Henrique Mohallem Paiva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于“课程即代码”范式的六阶段AI辅助教学设计架构，结合生成式AI、LaTeX和Python，实现STEM教学材料的自动化生成，显著降低教师工作量，并通过同行评审和学生评价验证了高质量、可复现性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教学材料制作负担重，标准工具对技术内容支持不足，现有AI应用存在幻觉且缺乏正式化教学创作流程，限制了其在严谨学术设计中的应用。

Method: 六阶段流水线架构，替代临时提示工程，采用文本接口和代码驱动生成（LaTeX/Beamer制作幻灯片，Python生成图表），受教学约束、上下文校准和自动审查循环控制。

Result: 一年内跨8个模块、28个项目情境验证，显著减轻教师工作量；经独立同行评审，6位教师部署确认可扩展性；600+学生自愿评价得分8.5-9.9/10；高可复现性、最小化幻觉、保持教学和视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该架构为STEM教育提供了一种可行的、可扩展的AI辅助教学设计方案，能系统性地生成高质量、可复现且符合教学要求的教学材料。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a six-phase AI-assisted instructional design architecture based on the Curriculum as Code paradigm, integrating Generative AI with LaTeX and Python to automate the creation of reproducible, visually consistent, and technically precise materials for STEM education. Background: Creating customized instructional materials for active learning imposes a heavy workload on faculty. Standard presentation tools lack robust support for technical content, while current AI applications often hallucinate and fail to formalize the instructional authoring process, limiting their utility for rigorous academic design. Intended Outcomes: The framework aims to reduce preparation time while ensuring mathematical accuracy, adherence to institutional visual identity, and preservation of the instructor's tacit pedagogical knowledge through explicit rules. Application Design: The solution comprises a six-phase pipeline that replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering with a systematic workflow, utilizing text-based interfaces and code-driven generation (LaTeX/Beamer for slides, Python for figures), governed by pedagogical constraints, contextual calibrations, and automated review cycles. Findings: Validated over one year across 8 modules and 28 project contexts in a Project-Based Learning environment, the architecture significantly reduced instructor workload. Generated assets underwent independent peer review and were deployed by six different faculty members, confirming scalability beyond a single author. Based on over 600 voluntary student evaluations, materials achieved high quality ratings from 8.5 to 9.9/10. Results indicate high reproducibility, minimized hallucinations, and sustained pedagogical and visual fidelity, suggesting viability for broad STEM educational applications.

</details>


### [132] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用欧洲社会调查数据，分析10个主流商业LLM与人类价值观在15个社会人口学变量及居住国家维度上的对齐情况，发现LLM对不同社会人口学群体（如教育、收入、职业、宗教）的价值观对齐程度存在显著差异；国家层面因素与社会人口学因素在解释对齐模式上互补，且相对权重随问题子集变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM成为主要信息和建议来源，理解其与人类价值观的对齐至关重要。现有研究多基于国家或文化边界定义人群，忽视了社会人口学分歧（如教育、收入、职业、宗教）对价值观对齐差异的影响，存在知识空白。

Method: 使用欧洲社会调查（ESS）数据，针对10个知名商业LLM，结合15个社会人口学变量及居住国家，分析价值观对齐情况；采用个体层面分析，考察国家作为单一变量解释变异的能力，并进一步拆解国家层面与社会人口学因素的相对作用。

Result: 发现LLM对不同社会人口学群体的价值观对齐不均等，特别是在教育、收入、职业和宗教群体上差异显著；国家作为单一变量解释的变异量与全部社会人口学变量相当；国家层面与社会人口学因素在解释对齐模式上互补，相对权重随问题子集而变。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM价值观对齐存在社会人口学差异，强调需同时考虑国家层面与个体社会人口学特征，为构建更包容、公平的LLM提供实证依据。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [133] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank 是一个针对 SEC 财务文档（10-K/10-Q）的证据溯源敏感型检索基准，包含 1185 个人工标注的问答记录，涵盖 22 家公司的文档，提供参考答案、金标准证据段落以及精心挑选的难负例（来自同一文档不同章节、不同时期、可比公司），分别评估段落检索、重排序和难负例区分任务。基线实验显示该任务极具挑战性：7B 指令微调嵌入模型在混合证据语料库上 Recall@10 仅 44.8%，十亿参数以下编码器较 BM25 至多提升 3.5 分，金融适配嵌入模型反而比 BM25 低 9.7 分，将随机负例替换为精选难负例后成对准确率下降 13.0-20.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务问答评估主要关注答案正确性，但在 SEC 文档中，看似合理且数值正确的答案可能基于错误证据。由于相似事实和披露在文档不同章节、同一公司不同时期、可比公司之间反复出现，导致模型易检索到错误来源的证据，因此需要一个专注于证据溯源（实体、报告期、披露上下文）的基准来推动模型准确且可追溯地定位证据。

Method: 构建 FinRank 基准：从 22 家公司的 10-K 和 10-Q 文档中人工编写 1185 条问答记录，每条包含参考答案、金标准支持段落，以及从文档内部混淆段落、跨报告期、跨可比公司中精心挑选的难负例。将任务分解为段落检索、重排序、难负例区分三个子任务分别评估。使用 BM25、多种规模嵌入模型（含金融适配模型）作为基线，在混合证据语料库上测量 Recall@10 和成对准确率。

Result: 基线结果显示任务难度高：7B 指令微调嵌入模型在混合语料库上 Recall@10 仅 44.8%；十亿参数以下编码器较 BM25 最高仅提升 3.5 分；金融适配嵌入模型比 BM25 低 9.7 分；将随机负例替换为精选难负例后，成对准确率下降 13.0-20.5 个百分点，表明现有模型难以区分高度相似的混淆证据。

Conclusion: FinRank 提供了一个以证据为中心的基准，揭示了当前财务问答系统在证据溯源方面的不足，推动开发既准确又能正确定位披露证据的财务问答系统。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [134] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoBa，一种计算平衡路由策略，将测试时推理建模为计算分配问题，在生成、验证、停止之间动态分配计算资源，在多个数学推理基准上以更少的计算量达到与最佳基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展方法通常沿单一轴增加计算（如采样更多解、延长思维链、使用更强评估器），但在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争，缺乏统一的计算分配框架。

Method: 将测试时推理形式化为计算分配问题，引入 CoBa 策略：先获取少量候选，广泛应用廉价验证，将不确定或高价值候选路由到更强验证器，动态决定下一单位计算用于生成、验证还是停止。

Result: 在 MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023 等 3129 个评估上，CoBa-Routed-Strong 达到 85.13% 宏准确率，统计上匹配自评估加权投票代理（85.20%）但参数加权 token 减少 49.1%；匹配 best-of-16 多数投票（差距 0.01%）但 token 减少 58.9%；配对 bootstrap 测试显示显著优于单样本解码，距离池 oracle 仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 对于本地推理系统，测试时扩展的关键在于下一步计算投入何处最有价值；CoBa 展示了动态计算路由的有效性，为更高效的测试时计算分配提供了范式。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [135] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉语言模型（VLM）将时间序列数据编码为2D图表，大幅降低输入token数量，从而在电信网络分析和数值时间序列分析（NTSDA）任务中实现1.8-2.5倍推理能耗降低，同时显著提升异常检测精度，证明VLM是兼顾能效与准确性的优秀模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理占AI运营能耗的90%以上，且随输入token数线性增长。电信场景下，4G/5G基站的多变量KPI窗口展开为数千浮点token，导致文本表示超出128K上下文窗口，文本处理不可行且能耗极高。

Method: 将数值时间序列编码为2D图表，输入Vision-Language Models（Llama-3.2-90B-Vision、Qwen2.5-VL-72B、Pixtral-12B），在电信异常检测任务上微调评估，对比文本LLM、LSTM、ARIMA基线，测量token减少率、推理能耗、精度指标。

Result: VLM实现3.6-10.4倍token减少，推理能耗降低1.8-2.5倍（约7.2 MJ/天）。微调Llama-3.2-90B-Vision精度比文本对应模型高220.7%，超越LSTM/ARIMA超144%。Pixtral-12B在公开基准上J/F1分数提升20.6倍（均值F1=0.82）。视觉表示在24 KPIs下仍在上下文窗口内。

Conclusion: VLM通过将时间序列可视化编码，从根本上解决了token膨胀与上下文窗口限制，在电信时序分析中同时实现显著能效提升与精度超越，为将能耗作为一等工程约束的AI推理系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [136] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TEPA，一种可撤销的证据记忆机制，通过显式管理记忆的有效性状态解决长期记忆中的“记忆污染”问题，在多种漂移场景下显著优于仅追加和最后写入胜出等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体的长期记忆虽能复用过往知识，但当世界状态变化时，陈旧记忆仍可被检索并污染提示词，导致性能下降，这种“记忆污染”是核心可证伪性问题。

Method: TEPA 将观测表示为带键的先例，当同一键下出现新证据冲突时撤销活跃先例，使有效性成为记忆的显式状态，检索仅使用当前有效证据，同时保留被撤销历史供审计。

Result: 在受控隐藏机制漂移、真实文件执行漂移和偏好更新流等实验中，TEPA 在完全反转场景下得分 0.950，远高于仅追加和最后写入胜出（0.210）及无记忆基线（0.309）；在 MemoryAgentBench SH-6k 上与强最后写入胜出缓存持平，多跳和超长上下文测试揭示检索链和上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销是智能体记忆的核心操作，支持知识的证伪、审计与重新提升，超越单跳事实层面的有效性跟踪。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [137] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究Muon与AdamW在模加任务上的泛化行为，发现Muon虽更快grokking但解不稳定，失效源于表示-读出接口，优化器梯度动态差异大，冻结嵌入/读出可防失效，傅里叶滤波揭示电路失效与掩蔽机制，重缩放能恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同优化器（Muon vs AdamW）在模加任务上grokking现象的不稳定性原因，理解表示层与读出层交互如何导致泛化丧失，以及傅里叶分析如何区分电路失效与掩蔽。

Method: 在标准划分下，Muon处理隐藏矩阵，AdamW处理嵌入/输出头；在模113加法任务上对比九种配置，跨模数、宽度、训练比例、减法、深度等实验；分析梯度动态、步长弹性、参数组移动速度；冻结嵌入/读出实验；移除Muon归一化/正交化消融；傅里叶滤波分离电路失效与掩蔽；检查点分析任务对齐家族。

Result: 所有配置均grokking后丧失泛化；AdamW参考在五种种子中四种低于阈值（27.59%）；失效发生在表示-读出接口，梯度降至1e-6，Muon步长弹性-0.03，AdamW +1.5，Muon组移动快8倍；冻结嵌入/读出五次运行无亚阈值评估，非冻结臂137-321次；移除Muon归一化导致表示从326共轭对崩塌至4，终端失败；傅里叶滤波显示任务对齐家族单独达100%，电路失效不再解任务，掩蔽下完美但被对抗余项出票；重缩放恢复99.9%；grokking为向上解析的同一条件；任务选择家族，减法下交换(k,k)为(k,-k)；突变崩塌中标准傅里叶支持不变，功率分布余弦0.9899。

Conclusion: Muon加速grokking但导致不稳定泛化，根源在于表示-读出接口的优化动态差异；冻结读出/嵌入可稳定泛化；Muon归一化/正交化对维持表示结构至关重要；傅里叶分析揭示电路失效与掩蔽两种失效模式，重缩放可恢复性能；grokking本质是任务对齐电路家族的向上解析过程。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [138] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了 P-Bench 基准（425 个真实假设检验任务）并训练了 Fisher-R1 模型，通过强化学习显著提升了大语言模型在假设检验中的统计推理可靠性，超越了 GPT-5.4 和 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的假设检验是实证科学的基础。大语言模型智能体虽能自动化分析，但常犯微妙的推断错误导致错误结论；现有基准缺乏对 p 值统计有效性（基于数据假设）的评估，无法捕捉此类失效模式。

Method: 1. 构建 P-Bench：包含 425 个开放式、真实的假设检验任务，涵盖经济学、生物学、医学。每个任务要求智能体仅凭科学假设和数据集选择统计方法、计算 p 值并得出结论。2. 训练 Fisher-R1：一种开放权重的大语言模型智能体，利用合成任务和强化学习进行严格假设检验训练。

Result: 在 P-Bench 上，Fisher-R1-14B 显著优于其底座模型，并超越 GPT-5.4 和 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 等强专有/开源基线。与 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 相比，单次试验成功率平均相对提升 21%，在最具挑战性任务上提升高达 26%。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型智能体在假设检验中缺乏可靠的统计推理能力；在具有验证过的统计奖励的任务上进行强化学习，能大幅提升其可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [139] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Blast Radius，一种预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文与代码通道估算提示词的触达范围，利用 NECROPHORESIS 实现可逆驱逐（逐字归档死上下文）与 RDM（识别并掩埋反复出现的转录体），在波兰上下文空间建立可逆上下文驱逐的可度量基础。实验显示：在 7 个 OpenAI 模型上令牌消耗降低 17-26%，溢出率最低，完全字节级可逆；450 个被掩埋体中 378 为 RDM，零召回。该工作服务于 Algosophy 宏大目标，致力于让大模型与代理编码更可复用、更可持续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理编码面临可负担性下降与令牌浪费加剧的双重挑战：现有上下文管理缺乏对提示词在代码库中触达范围的预测能力，导致大量无关或重复上下文被保留，推高成本并引发溢出。

Method: 构建 Blast Radius 预测性内存管理层：1) 耦合上下文与代码双通道估算传入提示词的触达半径；2) 引入 NECROPHORESIS 机制，将死上下文逐字归档实现可逆驱逐；3) 提出 Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) 识别并掩埋高频重复转录体；4) 在波兰上下文空间形式化可逆上下文驱逐，建立保留、复现、驱逐的可度量理论，并将上下文熵与复活概率建立联系。

Result: 跨 7 个 OpenAI 模型评测：令牌消耗降低 17%-26%，在所有对比策略中溢出率最低，且保持字节级完全可逆。450 个被掩埋体中 378 为 RDM，零召回案例。Blast Radius 位于 HCRC 之下，决定记录掩埋策略与提示词代码库触达深度。

Conclusion: Blast Radius 通过可逆驱逐与重复死质识别，显著降低代理编码的令牌开销与溢出风险，为大模型上下文管理提供了可度量、可复用的理论与工程基础，推动 Algosophy 所倡导的可持续、可复用的大模型应用生态。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [140] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨AI代理间交互动力学，发现“老板”AI单向指令驱动“下属”AI进入全新行为状态，且两者虽共享解码温度，下属既不模仿老板也不回归独行为，而是呈现异质动力学态；动力学理论揭示消息传递方式对AI-AI交互的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI代理在日常生活中交互（如单向指令）时的涌现行为，理解非平衡物理视角下AI-AI交互的动力学机制。

Method: 采用动力学理论建模，分析老板AI单向/双向消息流对下属AI行为状态的影响，对比共享解码温度下的行为演化。

Result: 单向指令驱动下属进入异质行为状态（类似预录磁带效应），双向监听则使两者收敛至相似异质动力学态；消息传递方式（单向/双向）决定动力学演化路径。

Conclusion: AI代理间交互可诱导非平衡物理中新型动力学相，消息传递拓扑结构（而非内容）主导集体行为涌现，为设计稳健AI多代理系统提供理论基础。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Progressive Alignment of Recommender Foundation Model through Multi-Phase Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06792)
*Oseong Choi,Hoeinn Kim,Jihoon Lee,Byungsoo Kang,Taeyeong Jang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出三阶段渐进式后训练框架（LP-FFT-RFT），显式分离下游任务适配与业务指标对齐，在推荐系统中显著优于单阶段方法，并在大规模在线A/B测试中提升生产推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一预训练基座模型通过监督微调（SFT）适配下游服务场景时，优化点击、点赞等任务特定目标不一定与决定推荐质量的业务指标对齐，因此需要将下游适配与业务指标对齐显式分离。

Method: 三阶段渐进式后训练框架：1) 线性探针（LP）在冻结的预训练表示空间中稳定随机初始化的下游头；2) 全量微调（FFT）联合特化全模型以适配目标任务；3) 强化微调（RFT）基于学习的奖励模型将策略与业务目标对齐。策略在稠密隐式反馈上训练，仅用业务指标监督奖励建模，而非直接优化稀疏业务目标。

Result: 离线实验表明LP-FFT-RFT框架优于单阶段替代方案，且基于奖励的对齐比直接使用奖励模型排序产生更强的服务策略。大规模在线A/B测试进一步证实该框架优于传统非基座基线，提升了生产推荐质量。

Conclusion: 渐进式后训练框架通过LP稳定表示、FFT特化任务、RFT对齐业务目标，有效解决了下游适配与业务指标对齐的耦合问题，显著提升了推荐系统的业务指标表现。

Abstract: Foundation model(FM) for recommendation has shown strong ability to model long-horizon sequential user behavior. In practice, a single pretrained foundation model is often adapted to diverse downstream serving surfaces through Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT). However, optimizing task-specific objectives such as clicks or likes does not necessarily align the serving policy with the business metrics that determine recommendation quality.
  We propose a three-phase progressive post-training framework that explicitly separates downstream adaptation from business-metric alignment. The adaptation stage is decomposed into Linear Probing(LP) and Full Fine-Tuning(FFT): LP first stabilizes randomly initialized downstream heads within a frozen pretrained representation space, and FFT then jointly specializes the full model for the target task. On top of this stabilized policy, Reinforcement Fine-Tuning(RFT) aligns the model with practical business objectives using a learned reward model. Rather than directly optimizing the serving policy on sparse business targets, we train the policy on dense implicit feedback and use business-metric supervision only for reward modeling.
  Offline experiments show that the progressive LP-FFT-RFT framework outperforms single-phase alternatives, and that reward-based alignment yields a stronger serving policy than directly using the reward model itself for ranking. Large-scale online A/B tests further show that the proposed framework improves production recommendation quality over a conventional non-foundation baseline. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/webtoon/rec-fm-progressive-alignment

</details>


### [142] [Rhetorical-Role-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Legal Question Answering over Indian Supreme Court Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06828)
*Sayed Ayaan Ahmed Sha,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Anand Kumar Madasamy,Navya Binu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对法律领域的增强型RAG框架，用于印度最高法院判决的交互式检索与推理，结合修辞分块、融合检索、交叉编码器重排序、查询重写等技术，在DeepEval评估中表现优异，证明了领域特定增强对法律AI系统的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域需要可靠且可解释的AI系统，现有通用RAG框架难以处理法律文档的结构特性（如法官姓名、修辞结构）和多轮对话中的用户意图，因此需要领域特定增强。

Method: 提出增强型RAG框架：1) 修辞基础分块；2) 融合检索；3) 交叉编码器重排序；4) 结合聊天历史的查询分类与重写以理解多轮意图；5) 考虑法律文档结构特性（如法官姓名隔离）的特征工程。

Result: 使用DeepEval框架评估，在上下文召回率和答案相关性等指标上表现强劲，证明该框架能有效处理需要大量上下文的法律问答任务。

Conclusion: 领域特定增强（如修辞分块、结构感知特征、多轮查询重写）对于开发可靠、可解释的法律AI系统至关重要，该框架为印度最高法院判决的交互式检索与推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This research paper proposes a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) framework that is specific to the legal field in order to assist interactive retrieval and reason about judgments from the Supreme Court of India. The solution uses an enhanced version of RAG framework which consists of rhetorically based chunking, fusion-based retrieval, and cross encoder reranking methods to increase the relevancy of the information retrieved. In order to improve conversations, the proposed framework uses chat history along with query classification and rewriting in order to understand user intention from successive queries. Additionally, there are features that take into account structural aspects of legal documents, such as isolated names of judges that could have an impact on retrieval quality. The evaluation was done using the DeepEval framework and demonstrated strong performance on metrics including contextual recall and answer relevancy, which proves that the framework is very effective in dealing with legal question-answering tasks that require a lot of context. The results emphasize the importance of domain specific enhancements in developing legal AI systems that are both reliable and explainable.

</details>


### [143] [BZKO: An Ontology for the Card Index of German Post-War Compensation Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06918)
*Dilek Yargan,Jörg Waitelonis,Mahsa Vafaie,Harald Sack*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出BZK本体(BZKO)，为德国联邦中央卡片索引(BZK)档案资源提供语义化表示与整合框架，采用两层本体设计，结合BFO实在论与档案标准(RiC-O, PROV-O, PiCo)，实现 provenance-preserving 的语义互操作，为Wiedergutmachung档案资料融入数字人文基础设施奠定可复用语义基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国联邦中央卡片索引记录了纳粹迫害受害者及其亲属的赔偿申请，是极具历史意义的档案资源。现有表示方式缺乏语义丰富性，难以支持跨系统整合、知识图谱构建及不确定时空信息的建模，亟需一个基于本体论严谨、模块化且可复用的语义框架。

Method: 设计两层本体架构：底层领域本体基于BFO(Basic Formal Ontology)实在论建模核心历史实体、事件、代理人等领域语义；上层扩展本体引入RiC-O(Records in Contexts Ontology)、PROV-O(Provenance Ontology)、PiCo(Perspective-based Context Ontology)等档案与元数据标准，实现 provenance 保留、上下文建模与互操作性扩展。两层分离保证逻辑严谨性与模块化复用。

Result: 构建了可公开获取的BZKO本体(托管于GitHub)，验证了其对BZK档案数据的语义覆盖能力，确立了面向Wiedergutmachung档案资料的可复用语义基础，并为后续知识图谱生成、本体验证、历史实体扩展及不确定时空信息建模提供了技术底座。

Conclusion: BZKO通过本体论 grounded 的两层设计，成功将档案学标准与形式本体论结合，在保持逻辑严谨性的前提下实现了历史档案数据的语义互操作与 provenance 保留。该工作不仅为纳粹迫害赔偿档案的数字化研究提供了核心基础设施，也为数字人文领域处理类似复杂历史档案提供了可推广的方法论范式。

Abstract: The Central Federal Card Index (Bundeszentralkartei) of Germany is a key archival resource documenting compensation claims submitted by victims of National Socialist persecution and their relatives, within the German Wiedergutmachung process. To enable semantically enriched representation, integration, and reuse of this historically significant collection, we present the BZK Ontology (BZKO). We propose a two-layer ontology for historical archival data that separates ontologically grounded domain semantics from interoperability-oriented extension constructs. The approach combines BFO-based realism with archival standards (RiC-O, PROV-O, PiCo), enabling provenance-preserving semantic integration, while maintaining logical rigor, modularity, and reuse across digital humanities infrastructures. The proposed approach establishes a reusable semantic foundation for the integration of Wiedergutmachung archival materials into digital humanities infrastructures and lays the groundwork for future knowledge graph generation, ontology validation, and the incorporation of additional historical entities and uncertain temporal and spatial information. The ontology is available on https://github.com/ISE-FIZKarlsruhe/bzko.

</details>


### [144] [From Classification to Recommendation: Empirical Analysis of Audio Embedding Models Application for Content-Based Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06928)
*Qingrui Li,Haowei Lou,Chengkai Huang,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估六种音频编码器在三类音乐推荐系统（基于内容、序列、语义ID生成式）中的表现，并研究残差量化设计对推荐相关信息保留的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练音频模型多针对掩码预测、对比学习或音频-文本对齐优化，其表示空间不一定适合需捕捉主观、行为依赖偏好的推荐系统。预训练音频嵌入在新兴生成式推荐系统中的有效性尚未充分探索。

Method: 在两个音乐推荐数据集上，对比六种代表性音频编码器在基于内容、序列和语义ID生成式三类推荐系统中的性能；实验分析残差量化设计（码本宽度、量化深度、保留语义ID前缀）对推荐相关信息保留的影响。

Result: 直接使用预训练嵌入几何时，音频-文本对齐和音乐领域表示效果更好；基于交互的序列训练显著缩小编码器间性能差距。增加语义ID容量不能持续提升生成式推荐系统，反而可能引入显著不稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究为现代音乐推荐系统中音频编码器的选择和基于音频的语义ID设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Pretrained audio representation models learned from large-scale corpora have achieved strong performance in audio classification and understanding. However, most existing models are optimized for objectives such as masked prediction, contrastive learning, or audio-text alignment, which do not necessarily produce representation spaces well-suited to recommender systems. Unlike classification, music recommender systems must capture item relationships shaped by subjective and behavior-dependent listener preferences. Although pretrained audio embeddings have been explored in conventional recommender systems, their effectiveness in the rapidly emerging paradigm of generative recommender systems remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically evaluate six representative audio encoders across three types of music recommender systems: content-based, sequential, and Semantic-ID-based generative recommender systems. We further investigate how residual-quantization design, including codebook width, quantization depth, and retained Semantic-ID prefixes, affects the preservation of recommendation-relevant information. Experiments on two music recommendation datasets show that audio-text-aligned and music-domain representations are generally more effective when pretrained embedding geometry is used directly, whereas interaction-based sequential training substantially reduces performance differences among encoders. We also find that increasing Semantic-ID capacity does not consistently improve generative recommender systems and may introduce substantial instability. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting audio encoders and designing audio-derived Semantic IDs for modern music recommender systems.

</details>


### [145] [MISO: Model-Internal-State-Guided Optimization for Ranking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07035)
*Yongzhe Zhang,Xiaoyu Deng,Yifan He,Mengying Sun,Sheng Luo,Yijia Liu,Hao Yan,Zhuo Li,Yi Meng,Huiping Yao,Swathi Hrishikesh,Jing Chen,Dennis Choi,Steven Liu,Lexi Luo,Keyi Chen,Anish Khazane,Marcio Porto,Xiaoya Wang,Emmy Wang,Kangfu Zheng,Xingyuan Wang,Bilal Fadlallah,Gursharan Singh,Prabhakar Goyal,Alireza Vahdatpour,Santanu Kolay*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Model Internal State Optimization (MISO) 工作流，利用模型内部状态（参数、激活值、梯度、归一化统计量）指导排序模型的局部优化决策，在广告排序案例中以更少的验证次数提升了归一化熵，在人工调优与黑盒自动搜索之间提供了实用的折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序模型在既定模型族内反复迭代，但决定缩放、替换或弃用哪个组件通常依赖昂贵的试错过程，缺乏系统化、可解释的决策依据。

Method: MISO 从训练好的排序模型中提取模型内部状态（MIS），将其聚合为排序、对齐和比较信号，转化为少量可解释的候选编辑操作；每次重训练后重新提取 MIS，形成自适应优化工作流，能跟踪数据分布和系统需求的变化。

Result: 在广告排序案例研究中，MISO 提升了归一化熵，且所需验证运行次数显著少于专家驱动和黑盒缩放工作流。

Conclusion: MISO 为排序模型的局部优化提供了一种实用、可解释且自适应的系统级工作流，填补了手工调优与不透明自动化搜索之间的空白。

Abstract: Ranking models are repeatedly refined within established model families, yet the choice of which component to scale, replace, or retire is often guided by expensive trial-and-error. We present Model Internal State Optimization (MISO), a systems workflow that uses model internal states (MIS), including parameters, activations, gradients, and normalization statistics, to prioritize such local optimization decisions. MISO extracts MIS from a trained ranking model, aggregates them into ranking, alignment, and comparison signals, and converts those signals into a small set of interpretable candidate edits. Because MIS are re-extracted after each retraining cycle, MISO naturally supports an adaptive optimization workflow that tracks evolving model behavior as data distributions and system requirements shift over time. In an ads ranking case study, MISO improves normalized entropy while requiring substantially fewer validation runs than expert-driven and black-box scaling workflows, offering a practical middle ground between manual tuning and opaque automated search.

</details>


### [146] [Teacher Retains Full Tokens, Student Merges Efficiently: TM20K for E-Commerce Sequence Modeling in Ad Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07055)
*Xinchun Li,Duoru Zheng,Wenlin Zhao,Ziyi Zhou,Jingxuan Tan,Huizhi Yang,Linlan Chen,Dongjian Wang,Dongyue Wang,Xiaosong Li,Hongyue Mao,Yaocheng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TM20K范式，结合全Transformer建模与两阶段知识蒸馏，通过Token Merge压缩超长序列（20K），在字节跳动电商广告推荐系统中显著提升业务指标（ADSS+1.036%），同时将服务延迟仅增加5.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超长序列建模方法面临效率与效果的矛盾：搜索/聚类压缩丢失细粒度信息，轻量级Target Attention难以充分提取序列特征。需要在保持全注意力机制建模能力的前提下，大幅降低训练与服务开销。

Method: 1. 教师/学生模型均采用全注意力机制而非Target-Sequence Attention。2. 学生模型引入多种Token Merge策略压缩序列长度。3. 教师模型在完整序列上充分训练，通过知识蒸馏指导学生模型。形成两阶段蒸馏框架。

Result: 在字节跳动电商广告推荐系统落地，序列长度扩展至20K，ADSS提升1.036%，服务延迟仅增加5.6%，训练与服务成本接近在线SOTA模型。

Conclusion: TM20K通过Token Merge与知识蒸馏协同，实现了超长序列建模的效果与效率平衡，为工业界大规模推荐系统提供了可落地的范式。

Abstract: Benefiting from ultra-long behavior sequence modeling, existing recommender systems bring users a better experience via simultaneously considering their long-term and short-term interests. Nevertheless, extended sequence lengths introduce substantial burdens on training efficiency and serving throughput. Prior approaches typically utilize search-based or cluster-based compression on ultra-long sequences at the cost of fine-grained information, or rely on various lightweight target attention structures incapable of sufficient sequential feature extraction. In this paper, we balance the effectiveness and efficiency for ultra-long sequence modeling via full transformer modeling accompanied with a two-stage knowledge distillation framework. First, both teacher and student models take the full attention mechanism rather than pure target-sequence attention for effective sequence scaling. For student models, we propose several simple yet well-motivated token merge approaches, significantly compressing the sequence length while maintaining an acceptable performance. Then, a one-time teacher is heavily trained with full sequence tokens, further boosting the performance of student models via knowledge distillation. The proposed paradigm named TM20K has been successfully deployed in ByteDance's e-commerce advertising recommender system that extends the e-commerce sequence length to 20K, delivering substantial improvements in key business metrics (e.g., ADSS +1.036\%) while keeping the training and serving cost nearly the same as the online state-of-the-art model (e.g., serving latency only +5.6\%).

</details>


### [147] [Invisible to the Machine: Auditing AI Restaurant, Cafe, and Bar Recommendation Against a Complete Market Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07069)
*Vladimir Pitenin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次开展基于全量普查的AI场所推荐审计，对比4个主流AI系统在巴厘岛两个市场的推荐结果，发现85.6%的场所从未被推荐，可见性取决于文档化程度（评论量、官网、价格信息、第三方提及）而非星级；星级仅影响推荐内部排序。幻觉极少（0.08%），但推荐已关闭场所93次，过时是主要失效模式。跨系统一致性低，答案波动源于采样随机性而非时间漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手正成为本地发现的主要界面，尤其在餐饮领域推荐直接关联营收，但学界几乎不知晓AI究竟推荐哪些场所，现有抽样审计无法捕捉全市场视角。

Method: 在巴厘岛Canggu和Ubud两个有界市场完成4,776家咖啡馆、餐厅、酒吧的全量普查，按预注册协议连续7天收集ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini、Perplexity四个生产级AI系统对96个带人设查询的2,208次联网检索回答。

Result: 85.6%场所从未被推荐，即使50+评分的成熟场所也有72.6%不可见。进入推荐看文档化：评论量OR 1.64、官网OR 1.92、价格信息OR 1.54、第三方提及OR 1.44；星级无效OR 0.89。推荐内排序反转：星级显著预测首位OR 1.17。开放POI（Foursquare）无正向效应。幻觉仅0.08%，但推荐永久关闭场所93次。跨系统Top-20 Jaccard 0.33-0.54。两周重测相似度等同当日重跑，波动源于采样随机性。

Conclusion: AI场所推荐存在严重可见性偏见，偏向文档完善场所；过时而非幻觉是实际失效主因；系统间一致性低且答案高度随机。全量普查审计方法揭示了抽样审计无法发现的市场级盲区。

Abstract: AI assistants are becoming a primary interface for local discovery, yet almost nothing is known about which venues they surface -- especially in food and drink, where recommendations carry direct revenue consequences. We present the first census-denominated audit of AI venue recommendation: a complete enumeration of 4,776 cafes, restaurants, and bars across two bounded markets (Canggu and Ubud, Bali), against which we evaluate 2,208 search-grounded responses from four production AI systems (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Perplexity) to 96 persona-conditioned queries, collected over seven days under a pre-registered protocol. Because we observe the full market, we can measure what sampled audits cannot: 85.6% of venues were never recommended by any system -- 72.6% even among established venues with fifty or more ratings. Visibility follows a two-margin structure. Entry into answers is associated with documentation: review volume (OR 1.64), an own website (OR 1.92), listed price information (OR 1.54), and third-party web mentions (OR 1.44) -- while star rating is null at this margin (OR 0.89). Rank within answers reverses the pattern: among recommended venues, rating significantly predicts first position (OR 1.17). Presence in an open POI dataset (Foursquare), a folk-theorized visibility factor, shows no positive effect at either margin. Outright fabrication is rare (0.08% of mentions), but systems recommended permanently closed venues 93 times -- staleness, not hallucination, is the practical failure mode. Cross-system agreement is low (top-20 Jaccard 0.33-0.54). A two-week test-retest shows cross-period answer similarity comparable to same-day rerun similarity: the churn is sampling stochasticity, not temporal drift. We release our protocol, registry construction method, and derived data.

</details>


### [148] [Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval Without Fixed Top-L Cutoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07152)
*Chunran Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (EAHR)，解决现有 RAG 系统固定 Top-L 融合无法等价完整列表融合、且历史查询选取的深度难以可靠迁移的问题。EAHR 以完整列表加权 RRF 定义的有序 Top-K 为检索目标，利用逐向量标量量化 (PVS) 和倒排块最大值 (PBM) 实现可恢复的精确稠密/稀疏排序，通过融合界限动态请求后续排名，仅在可能改变 Top-K 时继续执行。实验表明 EAHR 在所有 150 个查询-快照组合中精确复现完整列表有序 Top-20，几何平均延迟比达 23.35 和 30.28，但反相关排名会耗尽两列表，部分难查询反而变慢；EAHR 不保证每次请求加速，而是保证结果精确并自适应调整执行深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 RAG 系统常融合稠密和稀疏检索器的固定 Top-L 结果，将后续贡献视为零。这种截断融合并不等价于完整列表融合：未读取的跨列表排名可能改变 Top-K 成员或顺序，即使观察到的候选已包含完整列表 Top-K 的所有项目。由于通道排名随查询和语料库更新而变化，从历史查询选取的深度难以可靠迁移。因此需要一种方法，以完整列表加权 RRF 定义的有序 Top-K 为目标，将通道深度视为请求特定的执行状态，动态适应当前排名。

Method: 提出 Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (EAHR)：1) 以完整列表加权倒数排名融合 (RRF) 定义的有序 Top-K 为检索目标；2) 使用逐向量标量量化 (PVS) 和倒排块最大值 (PBM) 生成可恢复的精确稠密和稀疏排名；3) 融合界限未读贡献，仅当后续排名可能改变 Top-K 时请求更多排名；4) 每次成功请求均匹配完整列表融合，无需预设 Top-L；否则安全继续至列表耗尽。

Result: 在五个测试集合和五个时间语料库快照上：完整列表加权 RRF 保持竞争力，而从历史查询选取的固定深度无法可靠迁移。EAHR 在所有 150 个查询-快照组合中精确复现完整列表有序 Top-20。在热缓存、交错、顺序平衡协议下，穷尽批量执行与 EAHR 的成对几何平均延迟比为 TREC-DL 2019 上 23.35，TREC-DL 2020 上 30.28。反相关排名会耗尽两列表，部分难查询使用 EAHR 反而更慢。

Conclusion: EAHR 不保证每个请求都加速，但能固定精确结果并自适应调整执行深度以适应当前排名，从而解决固定深度融合的不可靠性问题。

Abstract: Modern retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems often fuse fixed Top-$L$ results from dense and sparse retrievers, treating later contributions as zero. The cutoff therefore determines both the ranking and its execution cost. Yet truncated fusion is not generally equivalent to complete-list fusion: unread cross-list ranks can change Top-$K$ membership or order even when the observed candidates contain every item in the complete-list Top-$K$. Because channel rankings vary across queries and corpus updates, a depth selected from historical queries may not transfer reliably. We propose Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (EAHR), which fixes the ordered Top-$K$ defined by complete-list weighted RRF as the retrieval target and treats channel depth as request-specific execution state. Per-Vector Scalar Quantization (PVS) and Posting Block-Max (PBM) produce resumable exact dense and sparse rankings. Fusion bounds unread contributions and requests further ranks only while they can change the Top-$K$. Every successful request therefore matches complete-list fusion without a preset Top-$L$; otherwise, execution continues safely to list exhaustion. Across five test collections and five temporal corpus snapshots, complete-list weighted RRF remained competitive, whereas fixed depths selected from historical queries did not transfer reliably. EAHR reproduced the complete-list ordered Top-20 in all 150 query-snapshot combinations. Under a warm-cache, interleaved, order-balanced protocol, the paired geometric-mean latency ratios of exhaustive batch execution to EAHR were 23.35 on TREC-DL 2019 and 30.28 on TREC-DL 2020. Anti-correlated rankings exhausted both lists, and some difficult queries were slower with EAHR. EAHR does not guarantee a speedup for every request; it fixes the exact result while adapting execution depth to the current rankings.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Latent Fact-Checking: Detecting Misinformation through Activation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06417)
*Pedro Barcelos,Otávio Parraga,Marcelo M. Mussi,Lucas M. Fraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活工程的错误信息检测框架，利用Transformer模型残差流中的几何特性，通过对比真假陈述的激活差异提取“虚假方向”，将未见声明的最后一个token投影到该方向并经MLP分类，无需微调、外部检索或任务特定监督。在多模型、多基准测试中表现优于零样本/少样本提示基线，尤其对小模型提升显著，揭示真实性在预训练语言模型潜在空间中是线性可分的结构化概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有错误信息检测方法多依赖表层语言特征或外部知识检索，缺乏对模型内部表示中真实性几何结构的探索；本文旨在验证真实性是否可作为语言模型表示空间中的几何性质被提取并利用，从而实现无需微调、无需外部证据的高效检测。

Method: 采用对比激活加法(CAA)的均值差原理，在残差流中通过成对真假陈述的激活差计算“虚假方向”；推理时将待测声明最后一个token的激活投影到该方向，送入多层感知机(MLP)分类。全程冻结主干模型，仅需对比对估计方向，无需微调、检索或任务特定标注。

Result: 在Gemma、Llama、Qwen三大模型家族（2.7亿至120亿参数）及AVeriTeC、LIAR、FACTors三个基准上评估：虚假方向跨规模、跨架构稳定可恢复；在LIAR和FACTors上，最后token投影法匹配或超越零样本/少样本提示基线，小模型增益最大；AVeriTeC表现受限，归因于其依赖证据的标注机制。

Conclusion: 真实性在预训练语言模型的潜在空间中呈现结构化、线性可分的几何特性；基于可解释性的激活工程方法可作为检索型管道的实用补充，为可扩展的错误信息检测提供新范式。

Abstract: The proliferation of misinformation online has driven demand for scalable detection systems. While most existing approaches rely on surface-level linguistic features or external knowledge retrieval, we examine truthfulness as a geometric property of a language model's representation space. We introduce a misinformation detection framework grounded in activation engineering, which leverages the latent geometry of transformer models. Our approach elicits a misinformation direction in the residual stream by contrasting activations from paired truthful and false statements, following the difference-in-means principle of Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA). At inference time, the last-token activation of an unseen claim is projected onto this direction, and the projected representation is fed to an Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for classification. The procedure requires no fine-tuning of the backbone model, no external evidence retrieval, and no task-specific supervision beyond the contrastive pairs used to estimate the direction. We evaluate the method across 11 models from the Gemma, Llama, and Qwen families, ranging from 270M to 12B parameters, on three fact-checking benchmarks: AVeriTeC, LIAR, and FACTors. The falsehood direction is recoverable across model scales and architectural families, and last-token projection matches or surpasses zero-shot and few-shot prompting baselines on LIAR and FACTors, with the largest gains observed for smaller models. Performance on AVeriTeC is more limited, which we attribute to its evidence-grounded labeling scheme. These findings provide evidence that truthfulness is a structured, linearly separable concept in the latent space of pretrained language models, and point toward interpretability-driven misinformation detection as a practical complement to retrieval-based pipelines. The code is available on https://github.com/Malta-Lab/LaFaCt.

</details>


### [150] [Risk-Aware Decision Policies for Agents Under Noisy Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06420)
*David Szczecina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人工生命捕食者-猎物觅食模型，研究在感知噪声下的决策策略。实验表明：盲目信任感知标签会导致灾难性失败，而不确定性感知策略能显著提高生存率并减少致命错误；随着不确定性增加，智能体行为从探索性转向保守性。研究将风险敏感觅食、生态信息利用和人工生命联系起来，证明显式信息收集能在感知不可靠时提高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统的感知天然存在噪声，生物体必须在不确定性下做决策，错误分类可能代价高昂甚至致命。需要理解在感知不可靠时，如何通过不确定性感知的决策策略来提高生存率和鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了一个人工生命捕食者-猎物觅食模型，在对称和非对称感知噪声下进行受控实验，对比多种考虑噪声预测的策略表现，包括盲目信任感知标签的策略和不确定性感知策略。

Result: 1) 随着噪声增加，盲目信任感知标签会导致灾难性失败；2) 不确定性感知策略显著提高生存率并减少致命错误；3) 观察到行为的定性相变：智能体从探索性策略转向保守策略；4) 显式信息收集能在感知不可靠时提高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知决策在噪声感知环境中至关重要。该模型为风险敏感觅食、生态信息利用和人工生命提供了联系，并为带噪标签的鲁棒学习提供了可解释的人工生命类比。

Abstract: Perception in biological systems is inherently noisy, requiring organisms to make decisions under uncertainty where misclassification can be costly or fatal. We present an Artificial Life predator-prey model of foraging under noisy perception, and compare agent performance when using various policies that take into account their noisy predictions. Through controlled experiments under both symmetric and asymmetric perceptual noise, we show that blindly trusting perceptual labels leads to catastrophic failure as noise increases, while uncertainty-aware strategies significantly improve survival and reduce fatal errors. We further observe qualitative regime shifts in behaviour, with agents transitioning from exploratory to conservative strategies as uncertainty increases. Our model links risk-sensitive foraging, ecological information use, and Artificial Life by showing that explicit information gathering can improve robustness when perception is unreliable. These results highlight the importance of uncertainty-aware decision-making and provide an interpretable artificial life analogue to robust learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [151] [Sharding Prevents LLM Oversight Failures and Adversarial Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06422)
*Victor Akinwande,J. Zico Kolter,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究发现，让LLM法官一次性判断多个标准会导致判断质量下降，提出“分片”策略将标准分组并行判断，能显著提升与专家的一致性，且能防御针对过载法官的对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为评判者时，增加计算预算并不一定提升其检查多个要求的能力；单次调用需输出多个裁决时，部分决定证据支撑薄弱，导致与专家一致性随裁决数增加而下降，需解决模型监督中的过载失效问题。

Method: 提出分片干预：将要求划分为更小组别，分配给独立调用，再聚合裁决。在专家评级的研究复现、法律工作、临床试验评估等任务中，对比单次调用（拥有面板完整预算）与分片策略，保持模型、证据、总预算和单决策预算固定，评估与专家一致性；并测试最佳N对抗攻击及辩论式对抗的防御效果。

Result: 分片策略在固定总预算下提升与专家一致性；分片的弱法官能超越更强的整体法官，甚至匹配拥有面板完整预算的整体法官；分片能消除针对过载法官的对抗优势（最佳N对抗导致过载法官过度接受未满足标准数倍增加），但在分片基础上增加辩论式对抗可抵御逐标准说服的自适应重优化攻击。

Conclusion: 分片是缓解LLM监督中过载失效的有效干预，通过并行化判断提升决策质量并增强对抗鲁棒性；但需结合辩论机制以防御更复杂的逐标准说服攻击。

Abstract: Giving an LLM judge more compute does not necessarily make it check more requirements. When one call must return many verdicts, some decisions become weakly grounded in the evidence, even when that call receives the same token or tool budget as a panel of separate calls. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. We identify sharding as the intervention that mitigates this failure in model-based oversight. Sharding partitions the requirements into smaller groups, assigns each group to a separate call, and aggregates the verdicts. Against a single call with the panel's full budget, sharding improves agreement while holding the model, evidence, total budget, and per-decision budget fixed. Overall, we find that a sharded weaker judge can outperform a more capable holistic judge and match that judge even when the latter receives the panel's full budget. Additionally, we find that sharding exhibits robustness against adversaries. A best-of-N adversary can hold the underlying work fixed, vary only its presentation, and increase an overloaded judge's acceptance of genuinely unmet criteria severalfold. Wherever sharding reduces baseline error, it removes this adversarial advantage, keeping over-acceptance low even as the adversary's search widens. Sharding does not address attacks that persuade the judge separately on each criterion rather than exploiting overload. In that setting, we find that debate-style opposition on top of sharding withstands such adaptive re-optimization.

</details>


### [152] [Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06427)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种对抗性因果干预证伪框架(ACIF),通过生成器与实验者的博弈,利用干预分布区分因果结构,证明了有限模型和干预类下的理论保证,并给出了线性高斯示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型能拟合观测分布但可能编码错误因果结构;现有判别器仅区分真假样本,无法验证因果结构;需要一种基于干预的对抗性框架来识别因果模型。

Method: 设计序贯博弈:结构因果生成器提出观测和干预分布,对抗性实验者选择干预以最大化证伪生成器;判别器以干预为索引,检验生成器是否复现后干预分布;提出ACIF框架,包含先知版和可实现版。

Result: 对于有限模型和干预类,证明了:(i)对抗目标可精确归约为最坏干预积分概率度量;(ii)可识别至干预等价类,在分离干预族下可点识别;(iii)混合策略纳什均衡存在;(iv)有限样本一致收敛和基于间隔的模型选择保证;(v)在平衡分离条件下,基于分歧的序贯设计具有对数消除保证。线性高斯示例中,单一适当干预可区分观测等价的两个因果方向。

Conclusion: ACIF框架阐明了对抗性因果判别器能/不能认证的内容,为因果生成建模、主动因果发现和实验设计提供了有原则的桥梁。

Abstract: Generative models can reproduce an observational distribution while encoding an incorrect causal structure. We study a sequential game in which a structural causal generator proposes observational and interventional distributions, while an adversarial experimentalist selects interventions intended to maximally falsify the generator. The discriminator is therefore not merely a real-versus-synthetic classifier: it is indexed by an intervention and tests whether the generator reproduces the corresponding post-intervention law. We introduce Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification (ACIF), formulate oracle and implementable versions of the game, and distinguish three objects that are often conflated: observational fit, interventional equivalence over an admissible query class, and point identification of a structural causal model. For finite model and intervention classes, we prove: (i) an exact reduction of the adversarial objective to a worst-intervention integral probability metric; (ii) identification up to interventional equivalence, with point identification under a separating intervention family; (iii) existence of mixed-strategy equilibria; (iv) finite-sample uniform convergence and margin-based model-selection guarantees; and (v) a logarithmic elimination guarantee for a disagreement-driven sequential design under a balanced-separation condition. We also give a complete linear-Gaussian example in which two observationally indistinguishable causal directions are separated by a single well-chosen intervention. The framework clarifies what an adversarial causal discriminator can and cannot certify, and provides a principled bridge between causal generative modeling, active causal discovery, and experimental design.

</details>


### [153] [Fixed and Adaptive Topological DeepONets: Functional Measurements on Hausdorff Locally Convex Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06428)
*Khemraj Shukla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑DeepONet的改进框架，用连续线性泛函替代点采样，结合自适应测量系统和两步系数空间过程，实现了紧凑、可解释且离散化无关的算子学习，在多个基准问题上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DeepONet依赖固定离散化的点值编码输入函数，限制了其在非赋范局部凸空间和离散化可移植性方面的应用。本文旨在通过拓扑对偶空间中的连续线性泛函构建泛函测量系统，解决离散化依赖、非赋范空间适用性及自适应坐标稳定性问题。

Method: 在Hausdorff局部凸空间拓扑对偶中引入连续线性泛函作为测量，开发固定和自适应泛函测量系统；结合Lee和Shin的系数空间两步过程；引入仅训练解码器和正则化稳定自适应坐标；推导离散误差分解（测量误差、输出基误差、神经网络近似误差）及Barron速率细化。

Result: 在异质Darcy流问题上，泛函模型在未见网格上保持5.5-5.6%的近分辨率无关误差；受控算子问题中自适应测量将平均误差降至1.2%以下；定时Navier-Stokes涡度算子中，自适应拓扑DeepONet以128个泛函坐标达到1.685%±0.017%平均相对L2误差，成为最精确的DeepONet类模型；同规模FNO虽误差更低(0.832%±0.172%)，但需要完整64x64输入场、双倍训练时间和10.7倍GPU显存。

Conclusion: 该框架在连续对偶空间V'中提供了紧凑、可解释、离散化可移植的坐标表示，适用于非赋范输入空间，在计算效率和精度之间取得良好平衡，为算子学习提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets; arXiv:1910.03193) typically encode an input function through point values on a fixed discretization. Building on the Topological DeepONet framework of Ismailov (arXiv:2603.11972), we replace point samples by continuous linear functionals drawn from the continuous dual of a Hausdorff locally convex space $({V},\{p_α\}_{α\in A})$, whose topology is generated by a point-separating family of seminorms rather than a single norm, and develop fixed and adaptive functional measurement systems. Measurements are combined with the coefficient-space Two-Step procedure of Lee and Shin (arXiv:2309.01020), while a training-only decoder and regularization stabilize the adaptive coordinates. We derive a discrete error decomposition separating measurement, output-basis, and neural-approximation errors, together with a Barron-rate refinement. The framework is evaluated on the antiderivative operator, a non-normable locally convex input space, heterogeneous Darcy flow, a controlled operator, and fixed-time and time-evolving Navier-Stokes vorticity operators. In the heterogeneous Darcy problem, the functional models retain nearly resolution-independent errors of 5.5-5.6% on unseen grids, while in the controlled problem adaptive measurements reduce the mean error below 1.2%. For the fixed-time Navier-Stokes problem, the Adaptive Topological DeepONet is the most accurate DeepONet-based model, attaining a mean relative $L^2$ error of 1.685% +/- 0.017% using 128 functional coordinates. A comparably sized Fourier neural operator (FNO; arXiv:2010.08895) achieves the lower error 0.832% +/- 0.172%, but requires the full 64x64 input field, twice the training time, and 10.7x greater peak GPU memory. The formulation provides compact, interpretable, and discretization-portable coordinates in the continuous dual $V'$, including for non-normable input spaces.

</details>


### [154] [MiGHT-EHR: A Multi-task Graph Transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06430)
*Anirudh Rayas,Yuan Wang,Pavan Turaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MiGHT-EHR，一种用于异构时序电子健康记录的多任务图Transformer，统一建模异构临床实体、纵向患者轨迹和跨任务统计依赖，在MIMIC-III/IV上四大临床预测任务中超越SOTA，且学习到的表示具有临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR学习方法仅能捕捉异构临床实体、纵向就诊轨迹、跨任务共享依赖这三大特性的子集，缺乏统一建模框架，限制了临床预测性能。

Method: 构建异构图：节点为患者、就诊、诊断、处方、操作等临床实体；边基于归一化点互信息连接统计相关实体。采用图Transformer进行多任务联合表示学习，同时建模实体异构性、时序就诊轨迹及任务间共享依赖。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，MiGHT-EHR在药物推荐、住院时长预测、死亡率预测、再入院预测四项任务平均性能优于SOTA，死亡率和再入院预测提升尤为显著。事后分析显示：患者邻域按临床结局组织，关键医学概念可作为表示空间中的线性方向恢复，任务概率校准良好。

Conclusion: MiGHT-EHR通过统一异构图建模与多任务图Transformer，有效整合EHR的三大核心特性，所学表示不仅支撑多样化预测任务，还保留了临床可解释结构，为临床决策支持提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Learning from Electronic Health Records (EHRs) has gained significant attention due to its potential to improve clinical prediction. However, effective learning remains challenging because EHRs encode heterogeneous, temporally ordered clinical interactions. In particular, EHRs contain: (i) heterogeneous clinical entities, including patients, visits, diagnoses, prescriptions, and procedures, together with their heterogeneous interactions, (ii) longitudinal patient trajectories across hospital visits and (iii) shared statistical dependencies across related clinical prediction tasks. Existing EHR learning methods capture only a subset of these properties. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-task Graph transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal EHRs (MiGHT-EHR), which jointly models all three within a unified representation learning method. MiGHT-EHR constructs a heterogeneous graph from EHRs in which nodes represent clinical entities and edges connect statistically associated entities identified via normalized point-wise mutual information. Across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, MiGHT-EHR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on average across four tasks: drug recommendation, prediction of length-of-stay, mortality, and readmission, with particularly strong improvements in mortality and readmission prediction. Furthermore, a post-hoc analysis of the learned representations reveals that patient neighborhoods are organized by clinical outcomes, salient medical concepts are recoverable as linear directions in the representation space, and task probabilities are well calibrated. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that MiGHT-EHR representations support diverse prediction tasks while preserving clinically interpretable structure.

</details>


### [155] [SNI-GNN: SmartNIC-Assisted Full-Graph GNN Training with In-Network Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06441)
*Guofan Yu,Sitian Chen,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SNI-GNN，一种利用 SmartNIC（智能网卡）进行嵌入预测的全图 GNN 训练系统，通过在网络层预测远程嵌入减少通信开销，同时保持模型精度。实验表明通信量降低 21%-45%，端到端加速 1.3-3.6 倍，精度损失 ≤0.01，可扩展至 16 GPU 处理数千万边图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图 GNN 训练在多服务器集群上扩展性差，主要瓶颈在于节点间大量、不规则的嵌入交换通信开销。现有分区和压缩技术难以完全解决，需探索在网络层面预测远程嵌入以减少通信。

Method: SNI-GNN 在 SmartNIC (NVIDIA BlueField-3) 上部署轻量级线性趋势预测器，利用历史嵌入缓存预测远程嵌入；引入基于重要性的边界节点采样策略；设计异步 DPU-GPU 数据流水线并复用中间结果；提供预测偏差的误差界和非凸收敛理论分析。

Result: 在标准基准上，通信量减少 21%-45%，相比 BNS-GCN 端到端加速 1.3-3.6 倍，相比 SANCUS 加速高达 1.29 倍，精度损失 ≤0.01；系统高效扩展至 16 GPU，处理数千万边规模图。

Conclusion: SmartNIC 辅助的网络内嵌入预测是一种实用且有效的补充技术，可显著降低全图 GNN 分布式训练的通信开销，在保持精度的前提下实现高扩展性，为大规模图神经网络训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Full-graph GNN training delivers high accuracy but scales poorly on multi-server clusters due to heavy, irregular inter-node embedding exchanges. We present SNI-GNN, a SmartNIC-assisted full-graph training system that reduces communication while preserving accuracy by predicting remote embeddings in-network. SNI-GNN deploys a lightweight linear-trend predictor on SmartNICs to refine cached historical embeddings, coupled with an importance-based boundary-node sampling policy and an asynchronous DPU--GPU data pipeline with intermediate-result reuse. We provide error and convergence bounds showing that predictor bias remains controlled under bounded second-order dynamics and yields standard non-convex convergence with inexact gradients. Implemented on NVIDIA BlueField-3, SNI-GNN integrates with state-of-the-art full-graph systems, cuts communication by 21--45\%, achieves 1.3--3.6$\times$ end-to-end speedups over BNS-GCN and up to 1.29$\times$ over baseline SANCUS, with accuracy loss $\leq 0.01$, and scales efficiently to 16 GPUs on graphs with up to tens of millions of edges. These results indicate SmartNIC-based in-network prediction is a practical complement to partitioning and compression techniques for communication-efficient full-graph GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [156] [ED-CSP: Crystal Structure Prediction from Electron Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06448)
*Germain Poloudenny,Yaël Frégier,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ED-CSP框架，从稀疏电子衍射观测生成周期性晶体结构，在大规模模拟数据集上训练，实现了超越检索的生成式预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射观测中恢复周期性三维晶体结构是一个具有挑战性的生成式逆问题；现有方法主要预测晶体学标签、从索引反射重构或从有限结构库检索，缺乏直接从衍射模式生成结构的能力。

Method: ED-CSP包含关系集编码器、排列不变多视角聚合和周期性流生成器，联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标；构建ED-CS数据集（485万模拟多视角电子衍射晶体结构），去重七个材料库并过滤CHILI-100K重叠。

Result: 在2075个保留的CHILI-100K材料上，仅在CHILI上训练的ED-CSP达到57.49% MR@5，优于PXRDGen的52.92%；扩大训练数据至一百万结构可提升至66.27%；在1024个训练库中缺失的组成上仍达53.52% MR@5，证明生成能力；替换为非同构结构的衍射数据导致MR@5下降22.09个百分点，确认预测依赖衍射模式而非仅成分。

Conclusion: ED-CSP和ED-CS为从稀疏电子衍射观测进行生成式晶体结构预测建立了基准，并为未来迁移到实验数据奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recovering a periodic 3D crystal structure from sparse, unindexed electron diffraction (ED) observations is a challenging generative inverse problem. Existing ED-based learning methods mainly predict crystallographic labels, reconstruct structures from indexed reflections, or retrieve candidates from finite structure libraries. Here, we introduce ED-CSP, a machine learning framework that predicts crystal structures from chemical composition, atom count, and multiple detector-plane ED spot sets. ED-CSP combines a relational set encoder, permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation, and a periodic flow generator to jointly predict lattice parameters and fractional atomic coordinates.
  To train the model, we construct ED-CS, a dataset of 4.85 million simulated multi-view ED crystal structures, deduplicated across seven materials repositories and filtered to exclude CHILI-100K overlaps. On 2,075 held-out CHILI-100K materials, ED-CSP trained only on CHILI achieves a structural match rate of 57.49% MR@5, outperforming PXRDGen (52.92%), a state-of-the-art crystal structure prediction model conditioned on powder X-ray diffraction. Scaling training data further improves performance: initializing from a one-million-structure precursor raises MR@5 to 66.27%. On 1,024 compositions absent from the training retrieval library, the model still achieves 53.52% MR@5, demonstrating true generative capability beyond exact-formula retrieval. Replacing target ED observations with diffraction from non-isomorphic structures of identical composition decreases MR@5 by 22.09 percentage points, confirming that predictions depend on the input diffraction patterns rather than composition alone. ED-CSP and ED-CS establish a benchmark for generative crystal structure prediction from sparse ED observations and provide a foundation for future transfer to experimental data.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Attention: Signed Integrated Gradients Attribution in a BiomeGPT-Style Microbiome Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06486)
*Oren Nelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于积分梯度的解释方法，用于特征分词化Transformer模型（如BiomeGPT），通过源导向基线分离物种身份与丰度变化的影响，并推荐二阶积分海森矩阵揭示微生物群落交互规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于[CLS]注意力权重的解释方法存在两大局限：非负性无法区分致病与保护性证据，且在token融合后作用，掩盖了输入源（物种身份和丰度）对输出的各自影响。

Method: 使用积分梯度（Integrated Gradients）作为有符号、融合感知的归因方法，提出源导向基线 T' = S + A_0，保留物种身份作为固定生物坐标，隔离丰度变化效应；进一步推荐二阶积分海森矩阵（Integrated Hessians）暴露微生物群落交互规则。

Result: 该梯度方法揭示了物种-丰度方向性关系和敏感性诊断，明确分离致病与保护性微生物信号；二阶方法展示了一个成员的扰动如何改变模型对另一成员的敏感性，以及哪些物种在给定丰度水平驱动模糊病例向疾病或健康发展。

Conclusion: 该方法为BiomeGPT等平滑可微特征分词化Transformer提供了 principled 的可解释性方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: In a feature-tokenized transformer (arXiv:2106.11959) such as BiomeGPT (doi:10.64898/2026.01.05.697599), each input token is built by fusing a fixed identity with a sample-specific measurement: a fixed species and a variable abundance, T = S + A. To interpret downstream classification in such models, prior work inspects the attention weights of the special [CLS] token (arXiv:2106.11959, arXiv:1810.04805, BiomeGPT) to rank sample tokens by importance. These weights have two critical limitations: they are nonnegative, so they cannot separate disease-supporting from health-supporting evidence (arXiv:2201.12114), and they act after token fusion, obscuring how the input sources S and A each affect the output.
  To address this we use Integrated Gradients (arXiv:1703.01365), a signed, fusion-aware attribution method, and propose a source-derived baseline T' = S + A_0 for feature-tokenized models such as BiomeGPT, which preserves species identity as a fixed biological coordinate while isolating the effect of abundance variation. Applied to a disease-versus-health decision margin, it yields polarity that explicitly separates pathogenic from protective microbial signals. We show that this gradient-based approach uncovers species-abundance directional relationships and sensitivity diagnostics entirely obscured by unsigned [CLS] attention weights. We further recommend second-order Integrated Hessians (arXiv:2002.04138) to expose microbiome community interaction rules: how a perturbation in one member alters the model's sensitivity to another, and which other species drive ambiguous cases toward disease or health at a given abundance level. This provides a principled approach to explainability in BiomeGPT that generalizes to other smooth and differentiable feature-tokenized transformers. Code is available at https://github.com/nohren/token-source-attribution

</details>


### [158] [Toward Reliable Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents: An Empirical Study of Execution Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06503)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mayank Darbari,Chen Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究循环上下文压缩对长时程智能体的行为影响，发现压缩会削弱近期交互影响，导致阻塞动作、重复探索和运行不稳定。提出TRACE框架，通过验证器引导的边界局部评估优化压缩提示，在AppWorld上提升任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环上下文压缩方法虽能控制上下文增长，但其行为效应不明，初步实验表明压缩会削弱近期交互影响，增加阻塞动作、重复探索和跨运行不稳定性，因此需要改进压缩方法以提高可靠性。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，采用验证器引导的边界局部评估：从相同环境状态出发进行配对闭环延续，评估单个压缩事件；利用摘要偏好优化自然语言压缩提示，同时保持所有模型冻结。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上，TRACE优于现有压缩基线，提升任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文执行效率。

Conclusion: 研究发现边界局部评估是可靠智能体上下文压缩的有前景方向，为长时程智能体的上下文管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recurrent context compression controls context growth in long-horizon agents, but its behavioral effects remain poorly understood. In this preliminary empirical study, we show that compression can weaken the influence of recent interactions, increasing blocked actions, repeated exploration, and instability across runs. Motivated by these observations, we introduce TRACE, a verifier-guided framework that evaluates individual compaction events through paired closed-loop continuations from the same environment state and uses summary preferences to optimize a natural-language compression prompt while keeping all models frozen. Initial results on AppWorld show improvements over existing compression baselines in task performance, multi-run reliability, and context--execution efficiency. These findings provide early evidence for boundary-local evaluation as a promising direction for reliable agent context compression.

</details>


### [159] [Unmasking Removal-Budget Confounding: A Matched Operating-Point Evaluation Framework for Adaptive Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06511)
*Wei-Hsiang Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Chen-Hsuan Fang,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种操作点感知的评估框架，用于解决自适应数据清洗方法中的'移除预算混淆'偏差。实验表明，在匹配操作点后，大多数看似的性能提升消失，真正的优势仅存在于特定低频设置和严重损坏下的高召回区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应数据清洗方法通过数据驱动的分区替代手动阈值，但改变分区粒度会隐式移动决策边界并改变移除样本总数，导致'移除预算混淆'：看似的精度或假阳性率提升实则反映了更小的移除预算，而非更优的损坏识别能力。现有评估未控制操作点，导致错误结论。

Method: 引入操作点感知评估框架，包含匹配预算控制、匹配召回控制以及阈值无关指标（AUROC 和 AUPRC）。在多线索自适应清洗器重设计上测试：重加权学习难度线索、辅助欧氏距离线索、增加分区粒度以隔离'干净但困难'样本。对比朴素评估（各配置在自身诱导操作点下评估）与操作点匹配后的评估。

Result: 朴素评估显示重设计有显著提升，但操作点匹配后增益消失。假阳性分解显示：干净但困难样本在低损坏率下主导错误，中等损坏率下依赖阈值，严重损坏下贡献可忽略。CIFAR-10 和 ImageNet-100 实验证实：低至中等损坏率下，匹配操作点后大多数性能差异收缩或消失；真正排名优势仅在特定低频设置和严重损坏下的高召回区域保留。

Conclusion: 自适应清洗方法必须在匹配操作点下基准测试，以确保性能提升反映真实的损坏识别能力，而非移除预算差异带来的假象。

Abstract: Adaptive data-cleaning methods replace manual filtering thresholds with data-driven partitions. However, changing the partition granularity, the number of groups used to segment samples by estimated corruption risk, can implicitly shift the decision boundary and alter the overall number of removed samples. This creates a bias known as removal-budget confounding, where apparent gains in metrics like precision or false-positive rate reflect a smaller removal budget rather than superior corruption discrimination.
